Stay under 180 days or the visa dies \[[102](#o102)\].

**The 2026 cancellations.** Since July 2026 the UAE has cancelled residence visas of Bangladeshis who were home on leave, about 5,000 by mid-October, with no formal reason given; the counts and possible causes are in Background ("Is it open now") \[[N7](#n7), [N8](#n8), [N9](#n9)\]. Notices came by email, SMS or through the sponsor; some learned only at check-in or at UAE immigration, and some had visas with over a year left \[[N12](#n12), [N13](#n13), [N14](#n14), [N10](#n10), [N15](#n15)\]. In many cases the person had been away less than six months, so the visa had not lapsed under the 180-day rule; in those cases the main cause was cancellation through the sponsor \[[N10](#n10)\].

**Check before you fly.**
1. Abu Dhabi, Sharjah, Ajman, Umm Al Quwain, Ras Al Khaimah or Fujairah visa: ICP's "File Validity" page (smartservices.icp.gov.ae); choose "Residency", then give the Emirate Unified Number, file number or Emirates ID number with your nationality and date of birth, or search by "Passport Information"; ICP 600522222 \[[95](#o95), [96](#o96)\].
2. Dubai visa: GDRFA's status inquiry (smart.gdrfad.gov.ae); from abroad call +9714-313-9999 \[[95](#o95)\].


**If it was cancelled.**
1. **Email your details to the Bangladesh Embassy,** mission.abudhabi@mofa.gov.bd, with the subject "Visa Cancellation Issue - [your name]" \[[140](#o140)\]. You'll need \[[140](#o140)\]:
   - your name, profession, current location, mobile or WhatsApp number and email address;
   - your passport number and Emirates ID number, with copies of both;
   - the company or sponsor's name and mobile number;
   - the visa expiry date, and the cancellation date with a copy of the cancellation;
   - the date of your last visit to Bangladesh.

   If you already sent these, you need not send them again \[[140](#o140)\]. For urgent help call the Embassy, +971566117215, or the Dubai Consulate, +971508168253; from Bangladesh, the Probashi call centre, 16135 (+8809610102030 from abroad) \[[140](#o140), [141](#o141)\]. The Embassy forwarded 985 cases to the UAE by diplomatic note, but being listed does not guarantee reinstatement \[[N13](#n13), [N9](#n9)\].
2. Ask the employer whether it cancelled or can re-apply; the Embassy was checking with employers \[[N16](#n16)\].
3. Some who bought tickets were refused boarding because the airline system showed the visa invalid \[[N10](#n10)\]; fares rose to Tk 70,000-1 lakh (see Scams) \[[N15](#n15)\].
4. For unpaid wages or gratuity, email the labour wing (labourwingauh@gmail.com) \[[132](#o132)\]; an ILOE payout needs you to be in the UAE \[[38](#o38)\].

> **Need to know**
> - Has any cancelled Bangladeshi visa been reinstated, and by what process?
> - Can a worker whose visa was cancelled abroad still claim gratuity and unpaid wages from Bangladesh, and through whom?

### Bringing your family

A worker can sponsor a spouse and children only with a salary of at least AED 4,000 a month (about Tk 1.36 lakh), or AED 3,000 plus housing given by the employer, and housing big enough for the family; the steps, and the 2026 status for Bangladeshi sponsors, are under "Family residence" \[[142](#o142), [82 (2024)](#o82), [N43](#n43), [2](#o2)\]. For a short visit by a relative, see "Family visit visa".

### Scams

**Scams done to you.**

1. **A "free visa" or job that doesn't exist.** The visa is real but has no job behind it; you pay again to renew \[[N2](#n2)\].
   **Check it is real:** ask for the MOHRE offer letter and check it by reference number; check an entry permit on GDRFA Dubai (if issued in Dubai) or ICP (the other emirates) \[[6](#o6)\]. **Report it:** MOHRE, +971 6 802 7666 or ask@mohre.gov.ae \[[6](#o6)\]; in Bangladesh, 16135 or a written complaint to BMET (Step 1, "If an agency or broker takes your money") \[[68](#o68), [50](#o50)\].
2. **A fake or oversold smart card.** In 2022 cards were sold beyond the UAE permissions \[[N29 (2022)](#n29)\].
   **Check it is real:** on portal.wewb.gov.bd/bmet-wewbm-check (Step 6) \[[101](#o101)\]. **Report it:** 16135, or in writing to BMET's Director General (Step 1) \[[68](#o68), [50](#o50)\].
3. **"Your cancelled visa can be reinstated for a fee."** Only the UAE government can reactivate a visa; the embassy only collects details and passes them to the UAE government \[[N9](#n9)\]. In July 2026 the embassy warned that false information was circulating under the Bangladesh government's logo, the national flag and even the embassy's email address \[[N44](#n44), [N45](#n45)\].
   **Check it is real:** check the visa yourself on GDRFA (Dubai) or ICP (the other emirates), as in Step 5 \[[6](#o6)\]; trust only official sources \[[N45](#n45)\]. **Report it:** to Dubai Police on 901, its app or the eCrime platform \[[N34](#n34)\]; in Bangladesh, taking money on a false job promise is a crime: call 16135 \[[67 (2023)](#o67), [68](#o68)\].
4. **"Visa ban" or "visa open" posts.** The 2025 ban story came from a website that cheated customers \[[16](#o16)\].
   **Check it is real:** only the Embassy's own channels (Contacts) \[[143](#o143), [N45](#n45)\]. **Report it:** a fraudulent visa website to Dubai Police's app or eCrime platform \[[N34](#n34)\].
5. **Ticket gouging.** During the cancellations, Dhaka-UAE one-way fares of Tk 15,000-20,000 were sold for Tk 70,000 to 1 lakh \[[N15](#n15)\].

   > **Need to know**
   > - Where can a worker check the normal Dhaka-UAE fare and complain about an overpriced ticket?

**Scams done by the migrant.**
- Working on a visit or tourist visa: fines and legal liability \[[6](#o6)\]. Working for an outside company on a partner (investor) visa: in Abu Dhabi one worker got two months' jail and deportation, and the company was fined AED 50,000 \[[N3 (2019)](#n3)\].
- Fake certificates: the law minister says agencies submitted certificates from institutions that did not exist \[[N8](#n8)\].

## Work visa: free zone employee

Free zones are business areas with their own authority, such as Jebel Ali Free Zone (Jafza) in Dubai. A free-zone company applies for your work permit and residence visa to the free zone authority, and the number of visas it can get depends on its package or lease \[[36](#o36), [144 (2021)](#o144)\]. You are sponsored by the free zone authority, not the company \[[145](#o145)\]. Your job is governed by the free zone's rules together with the federal Labour Law; DIFC and ADGM, the financial free zones, have their own employment law \[[36](#o36)\].

1. **The company applies.** In Jafza, for example, it applies on the Dubai Trade portal with your passport copy and photo, and an attested degree only for managerial or professional jobs \[[146 (2024)](#o146)\]. The residence is valid up to three years \[[144 (2021)](#o144)\]. Jafza's normal package costs the company AED 2,378 for one year or AED 2,678 for two \[[146 (2024)](#o146)\].
2. **After you land,** the company reports your arrival and receives a DHA (Dubai Health Authority) medical form, your employment contract and medical and Emirates ID appointments; once you are fit it uploads your health insurance card and entry permit to finish the residence \[[147 (2024)](#o147)\].
3. **Work only in the free zone.** A Jafza-sponsored worker may work only inside the zone unless Jafza approves temporary outside work \[[144 (2021)](#o144)\].
4. **Check it is real** the same way as Step 5: Dubai free-zone entry permits on GDRFA, others on ICP \[[6](#o6)\].

> **Need to know**
> - Which free zones employ most Bangladeshi workers (Jafza, SAIF Zone, Hamriyah, RAKEZ, KEZAD)?
> - Does a free-zone worker file wage complaints with MOHRE or with the free zone authority?

## Work visa: domestic worker

Housemaids, cooks, nannies, gardeners, home nurses, family drivers and guards working in a household come under the domestic workers law (Federal Decree-Law No. 9 of 2022, amended by Decree-Law No. 21 of 2023), not the main labour law; it lists 19 such occupations \[[35 (2022)](#o35), [109](#o109)\]. Only offices licensed by MOHRE (the Ministry of Human Resources and Emiratisation) may recruit them, and the worker must be at least 18 \[[35 (2022)](#o35)\]. Bangladesh and the UAE signed a domestic worker MoU (memorandum of understanding) on 18 April 2018: recruitment goes only through MOHRE-licensed Tadbeer centres, only licensed Bangladeshi agencies may put forward workers, and the contract must state the job, salary, food, accommodation and transport, with salary paid through the WPS (Wage Protection System, the UAE's electronic salary transfer) \[[N46 (2018)](#n46), [28 (2018)](#o28)\]. In 2022 MOHRE announced that licensed "domestic worker recruitment offices" would replace Tadbeer centres; 138 such offices were active in July 2026 \[[N47 (2022)](#n47), [N48](#n48)\]. 

**Is it open now?** The route is open but tiny. Women going to the UAE fell from 24,307 in 2015 to 1,036 in 2024 \[[148](#o148)\]; BMET cleared 721 women for the UAE in 2025 and 764 from 1 January to 7 November 2026 \[[149](#o149), [52](#o52)\]. Of 23,044 Bangladeshis cleared for the UAE in 2026 to 7 November, 59 were "House Keeper", 30 "House Keeper Female", 9 "Housemaid" and 4 "Baby Sitter"; the 2,591 "Driver" clearances do not separate house drivers from company drivers \[[4](#o4)\]. For the general 2026 visa situation for Bangladeshis, see Background ("Is it open now").

> **Need to know**
> - Are UAE recruitment offices getting domestic worker entry permits for Bangladeshi nationals in November 2026, or are they refused at the visa stage?
> - What does a UAE housemaid or house-driver visa cost a Bangladeshi worker in 2026, and who sells it?

### Step 1: Check you qualify

1. **Age and passport.** The UAE minimum age is 18 and the maximum 60, and your passport must be valid at least three months \[[35 (2022)](#o35), [150](#o150)\]. For BMET clearance a woman going as a domestic worker must be 25 to 45 and bring proof of age and a no-objection letter from her legal guardian \[[50](#o50)\].
2. **Health.** The recruiting office has you medically examined no more than 30 days before you enter the UAE \[[35 (2022)](#o35)\]. The UAE residence medical includes a pregnancy test for domestic workers (maids, nannies, drivers and similar) \[[76](#o76)\]. Bangladesh's housekeeping course does not admit a woman who is pregnant, whose youngest child is under 6, or who has a contagious or incurable disease \[[51](#o51)\].
3. **Skill papers.** For jobs where the UAE requires a certificate, such as nursing and driving, the office must check you hold a certificate of experience \[[151 (2022)](#o151)\]. The office may also need a police certificate of good conduct from Bangladesh \[[151 (2022)](#o151)\].

### Step 2: Do the 30-day housekeeping course (women)

Housekeeping training is compulsory for women going to the Middle East as domestic workers and runs at 41 TTCs (Technical Training Centres, BMET's training schools) \[[50](#o50)\]. The course lasts one month (24 working days), is residential, and takes 10 to 40 women a batch \[[51](#o51)\].

You'll need \[[51](#o51)\]:

- age 25 to 45 (returnee women up to 50);
- class 5 pass (can be relaxed);
- your legal guardian's no-objection letter;
- a photocopy of your birth certificate, NID (national ID card) or passport, and the originals at the interview.

1. Watch for BMET's online admission notice, apply online, pay the fee and choose the TTC and the training date \[[51](#o51)\]. Fees are paid online into BMET's bank account; BMET sets them \[[51](#o51)\].
2. Go in person to the TTC for selection; the selection committee checks your originals and whether you can read and write \[[51](#o51)\].
3. Attend at least 90% of sessions \[[51](#o51)\]. You learn the destination's language, its home appliances and how to use them safely, and how to reach the nearest police station and the Bangladesh embassy \[[51](#o51)\].
4. Pass the final test (100 marks, pass mark 70%; language and practical work count 30 marks each) \[[51](#o51)\].
5. Download and print your digitally signed certificate from the online platform \[[51](#o51)\].

The certificate is valid 2 years for a first departure; between 2 and 3 years you need a 3-day refresher, after 3 years the full course again; a returnee with at least a year abroad does the 3-day refresher \[[51](#o51)\]. Ministry training branch: 02-41030254, dstraining@probashi.gov.bd \[[51](#o51)\]. TTC addresses: see Work visa (company worker), Step 1.

> **Need to know**
> - What is the housekeeping course fee in 2026?
> - Does the course include Arabic for the Gulf, and for how many hours?
> - Do men going as house drivers, cooks or gardeners need any specific BMET course?
> - Is the housekeeping course one month for women aged 25 to 45 whose youngest child is at least 6 (2023 policy \[[51](#o51)\]), or two months for women aged 25 to 40 whose youngest child is at least 5 (2024 Gulf curriculum \[[152 (2024)](#o152)\])?

### Step 3: Get the job through a licensed agency at both ends

1. **Bangladesh side.** Only agencies the ministry has approved may send women domestic workers \[[50](#o50), [153 (2024)](#o153)\]. Each must lodge a Tk 15 lakh FDR (fixed deposit) security with BMET, employ at least one woman in its office, take **no migration cost** from the woman, and sign an undertaking that its licence can be cancelled if she is abused \[[153 (2024)](#o153)\]. The ministry's order of 1 September 2024 approved 21 agencies, including Metak Al Madina Overseas (RL-2480), Just Recruiting (RL-2702), Tehjib Overseas (RL-1664), Raj International (RL-1523), Swapnasiri International (RL-2035) and Duranta Air International (RL-1876) \[[153 (2024)](#o153)\].
2. **UAE side.** Recruiting domestic workers without a MOHRE licence is a crime: up to a year in prison and/or a fine of AED 200,000 to 1,000,000 \[[35 (2022)](#o35)\]. A licensed office may deal only with licensed agencies abroad and must follow the MoU with the sending country \[[151 (2022)](#o151)\].

   **Check it is real:** ask for the UAE office's trade licence number, then search Services, Enquiry services, "Company Information" on mohre.gov.ae \[[N47 (2022)](#n47)\]; MOHRE's list of licensed offices is under Services, Authorised Service Centres \[[154 (2023)](#o154)\]. **The work permit:** the employer applies for your MOHRE domestic work permit with UAE Pass (the national digital ID) on MOHRE's website or app, at a service centre or through a Tawseel service provider; it is issued in 2 working days, free online apart from federal charges, and a centre's commission is capped at AED 72 \[[150](#o150)\]. A foreign-resident employer must prove a family income of at least AED 25,000 a month by a recent salary certificate (expatriate employer) and three months' bank statements (investor/employer); the permit lasts two years with a UAE citizen employer and one year with a resident employer \[[150](#o150)\]. For the Bangladesh agency's RL number, see Work visa (company worker), Step 1.

3. **Pay nothing.** A UAE office may not ask for or accept any commission or expense from you, before or after you start, directly or through anyone else, nor charge you for completing your employment papers \[[35 (2022)](#o35), [155 (2024)](#o155)\]. It must give a document saying it pays any commission abroad itself without charging you, and MOHRE tells offices to make sure brokers abroad charged the worker nothing \[[151 (2022)](#o151), [156 (2024)](#o156)\]. Your employer may not take money from you either \[[35 (2022)](#o35)\]. An office that collects extra money is fined AED 5,000 per case \[[151 (2022)](#o151)\].

   **What happens in practice:** many workers still pay fees at home and arrive in debt; the government registered 10,646 recruitment-fee complaints in 2025, across company and domestic work together \[[N49](#n49)\].

4. **Named ("direct") recruitment.** If a relative finds you a household, the office brings you by name at the employer's request; the office then skips some checks and owes the employer no refund if you leave \[[151 (2022)](#o151)\]. If you quit after probation for your own reasons, you pay your own ticket home (the employer pays if you cannot) \[[35 (2022)](#o35)\].

> **Need to know**
> - Which of the 21 approved agencies actually sent women to the UAE in 2025-26?
> - Does the MoU's list of approved Bangladeshi agencies still apply, and where is it published?

### Step 4: See the job offer and contract before you sign

1. The office must tell you the type of work and the wage before bringing you, let you read the job offer that matches the contract and sign it yourself \[[35 (2022)](#o35), [151 (2022)](#o151)\]. MOHRE tells offices that the agency in Bangladesh must explain every term in a language you understand \[[156 (2024)](#o156)\]. The office must also tell you your rights and where to turn if they are broken \[[155 (2024)](#o155)\].
2. Check the contract is on MOHRE's standard form: two years, renewable \[[35 (2022)](#o35)\]. It must state the workplace, the type of work, the start date, the total wage and basic wage, how you are paid, your leave, any probation, your rest periods and how it can end \[[35 (2022)](#o35)\]. It is in Arabic plus a language you understand \[[35 (2022)](#o35)\]; four copies are signed, one for you \[[156 (2024)](#o156)\].
3. The Embassy's labour wing in Abu Dhabi and the Consulate's in Dubai attest individual housemaid visas \[[60](#o60), [59 (2021)](#o59)\]. The Embassy's form states the basic salary in AED per month and makes the employer pay your ticket out and your ticket home \[[157](#o157)\].
4. Have BMET check the UAE visa online: free, 3 working days; bring the visa and a copy of the passport visa page \[[50](#o50)\]. At BMET clearance a single visa costs Tk 250 tax if attested, Tk 400 if not \[[50](#o50)\]. For the rest of BMET clearance, see Work visa (company worker). A Dubai maid-agency website gives Tadbeer minimum rates of AED 1,000-1,400 a month for general housemaids and AED 1,400-1,800 for nannies, and lists no Bangladeshi rate \[[N50](#n50)\].

> **Need to know**
> - What minimum monthly salary does the Embassy require before attesting a Bangladeshi housemaid visa in 2026 (it was Dh750 in 2014 \[[N51 (2014)](#n51)\])?
> - What do Bangladeshi housemaids in the UAE actually earn in 2026?

### Step 5: Arrive and get your residence visa

1. Wages are due from the day you enter the UAE \[[35 (2022)](#o35)\]. Probation can last up to six months \[[35 (2022)](#o35)\]. Under the "classic package" you are recruited onto the household's file and live with it for the two-year contract \[[155 (2024)](#o155)\]. Under the office's "temporary" (two-year) or "flexible" (hourly to yearly) packages you stay on the office's file: the office gets your residence visa and pays your salary, and flexible-package work is capped at 8 hours a day \[[155 (2024)](#o155)\].
2. The employer must insure you on its file before MOHRE issues your work permit \[[150](#o150)\], and must buy health insurance before your residence permit is issued or renewed (from 1 January 2025; mandatory in Abu Dhabi and Dubai, and being extended to the other five emirates) \[[31](#o31)\]; the basic package is AED 320 a year \[[31](#o31)\]. The employer's worker insurance covers up to AED 20,000 of unpaid wages (last 120 days), end-of-service pay, ticket home, work-injury costs and body repatriation \[[121](#o121)\].
3. Until the residence visa is stamped you cannot leave the country, except by cancelling the entry permit in an emergency \[[N52](#n52)\].
4. **Go through the residence steps** (the employer or office applies) \[[N52](#n52)\]:

   1. The employer gets your MOHRE domestic work permit online with UAE Pass (2 working days) and your entry permit \[[150](#o150), [N52](#n52)\].
   2. If you are already in the UAE, the employer files a change of status, valid up to 60 days \[[N52](#n52)\].
   3. **Medical fitness test** at an approved government health centre; it includes a pregnancy test \[[N52](#n52), [76](#o76)\]. The certificate is valid 90 days \[[N52](#n52)\].
   4. **Emirates ID:** give fingerprints (biometrics) within 10 days of the application, or it lapses \[[N52](#n52)\]. Track it at smartservices.icp.gov.ae with the application number starting 010 \[[83](#o83)\].
   5. The employer buys health insurance and prints the MOHRE contract; then the residence sticker goes in your passport \[[N52](#n52)\]. The Emirates ID application is valid 1 year with a foreign resident employer or 2 years with an Emirati one \[[N52](#n52)\].

> **Need to know**
> - Is the domestic worker's medical done within 30 days before entry (Executive Regulations) or within 30 days after entry (MOHRE recruitment manual)?

   All of this must be done within 60 days of entry, or fines apply \[[82 (2024)](#o82)\].

**Check it is real:** check your residence on ICP's File Validity page (choose "Residency") or, in Dubai, GDRFA's Visa Status page (see Work visa: company worker, Step 5) \[[95](#o95), [96](#o96)\].

### Your rights at work

These are minimums; any contract term worse than the law is void \[[35 (2022)](#o35)\].

- **Rest.** At least 12 hours' rest a day, 8 of them in a row \[[35 (2022)](#o35)\]. One paid day off a week, agreed with the employer; if you work it you get another day off or a day's wage, and not more than two rest days in two weeks unless you agree \[[35 (2022)](#o35), [151 (2022)](#o151)\].
- **Leave.** 30 days' paid annual leave, paid before you go; 2 days a month if you worked 6-12 months \[[35 (2022)](#o35)\]. A return ticket home every two years, a one-way ticket if the contract ends after the leave, and cash for unused leave when you finish \[[35 (2022)](#o35)\]. Sick leave up to 30 days a year with a medical report: the first 15 days on full pay \[[35 (2022)](#o35)\].
- **Pay.** In dirhams, within 10 days of the due date, proved by a written receipt \[[35 (2022)](#o35)\]. Deductions only for damage from your grave mistake, at most a quarter of the cost, with your consent or MOHRE's approval \[[35 (2022)](#o35)\]. WPS is compulsory only for private tutors, trainers, home care providers, private agricultural engineers and messengers \[[158 (2022)](#o158)\]; for housemaids it is voluntary \[[158 (2022)](#o158), [N49](#n49)\].
- **Food, room and care.** Suitable accommodation, meals and work clothes; medical treatment or health insurance; protective gloves and shoes \[[35 (2022)](#o35), [151 (2022)](#o151)\]. You may work for anyone else only through MOHRE's procedures, or for the employer's relatives (up to second degree) with your consent, and you may not be moved to another occupation without your consent \[[35 (2022)](#o35), [151 (2022)](#o151)\].
- **Your passport.** You keep your own passport and ID \[[35 (2022)](#o35), [109](#o109)\]. The fine on an employer who keeps them is only AED 500, and in practice it does not stop employers \[[151 (2022)](#o151), [N49](#n49)\].
- **Abuse.** Sexual harassment, forced labour and discrimination are banned \[[35 (2022)](#o35)\]. An employer whose household assaults or harasses you is fined AED 20,000, and MOHRE may suspend its file \[[151 (2022)](#o151)\].
- **Travel with the family.** The UAE contract still applies abroad unless that country's law says otherwise \[[35 (2022)](#o35)\].
- **End-of-service pay.** MOHRE says 14 days' wage per year after a year's service, lost if you stop work illegally \[[156 (2024)](#o156)\]; dues are paid within 10 days of the end \[[35 (2022)](#o35)\]. Domestic workers get no unemployment insurance (ILOE) \[[38](#o38)\].

> **Need to know**
> - Does any UAE rule give a domestic worker the right to keep and use her own mobile phone?
> - Has the Cabinet issued the end-of-service gratuity system for domestic workers promised in the 2022 law \[[35 (2022)](#o35)\]?
> - Does a domestic worker get half pay for days 16 to 30 of sick leave (the 2022 law \[[35 (2022)](#o35)\]) or no pay (MOHRE's domestic workers page \[[156 (2024)](#o156)\])?

### If you are mistreated or not paid



1. **MOHRE complaint.** You or the employer may complain during the contract and up to 3 months after it ends \[[159 (2022)](#o159), [35 (2022)](#o35)\]. Call 600590000, the Labour Claims and Advisory Centre on 80084, or use MOHRE's online domestic worker platform (eservices.mohre.gov.ae/Mohre.Domestic.UI) \[[156 (2024)](#o156), [137](#o137), [109](#o109)\]. A legal researcher hears both sides; if there is no settlement within two weeks MOHRE refers the case to court \[[151 (2022)](#o151), [109](#o109)\]. MOHRE itself decides claims up to AED 50,000, and its decision can be enforced; either side can go to court within 15 working days \[[35 (2022)](#o35)\]. Workers pay no court fees and cases are urgent \[[35 (2022)](#o35)\]. After referral you must file at the court within 14 days, and within 30 days you can ask for a temporary permit to work for someone else (not if you have been reported absconding); meanwhile the recruiting office must house and feed you \[[159 (2022)](#o159)\].
2. **If you are assaulted.** You may leave at once if the employer or their representative assaults, humiliates or sexually assaults you; report to the police promptly and notify MOHRE within 3 days \[[151 (2022)](#o151)\]. Then go back to your recruiting office, or give MOHRE another address and a phone number \[[151 (2022)](#o151)\]. If you leave without telling the employer for any reason, lodge a complaint within 48 hours, so MOHRE can protect you \[[159 (2022)](#o159), [156 (2024)](#o156)\].
3. **Police and shelters.** Police 999, ambulance 998 \[[156 (2024)](#o156)\]. Dubai Police: human trafficking hotline 8007283, labour complaints 8005005 \[[160](#o160)\]; its human rights directorate gives temporary shelter, visas and tickets home \[[160](#o160)\]. DFWAC (Dubai Foundation for Women and Children) runs a free 24/7 helpline, 800111, also on WhatsApp, with shelter, medical, legal, consular and immigration help \[[161](#o161), [160](#o160)\]. In Abu Dhabi, Ewa'a shelters victims of violence and trafficking; Sharjah's women's protection line is 800700 \[[160](#o160)\]. **In practice:** shelters need formal recognition as a trafficking victim, and police sometimes send women to their embassy instead \[[N49](#n49)\].
4. **The Bangladesh missions.** The Embassy in Abu Dhabi (Villa 46-48, Al Jawwalah Street, Al Saadah; +971-2-4465100; mission.abudhabi@mofa.gov.bd) takes complaints, attends labour courts and gives distressed workers money or a ticket home \[[143](#o143), [60](#o60)\]. The Consulate in Dubai's labour wing (weekdays 9 am-5 pm; WhatsApp text +971 50 816 8253 or +971 50 816 8363) counsels women workers and forwards complaints to Bangladesh \[[59 (2021)](#o59), [162](#o162)\]. Bangladesh runs no safe home for women in the UAE; its three are in Riyadh, Jeddah and Muscat \[[N53](#n53)\].
5. **From Bangladesh.** A complaint against the agency goes in writing, with evidence, to BMET's Director General: free, 30 working days; Director (Employment) 01711111544, diremi@bmet.gov.bd \[[50](#o50)\]. A woman who returns within a year after abuse gets Tk 5,000 at the airport welfare desk \[[50](#o50)\].

> **Need to know**
> - Does the Embassy or Consulate shelter a woman who escapes an employer, and where does she stay while her case runs?
> - Which language lines does MOHRE's 600590000 offer (is Bangla available)?

### Leaving or changing employer

1. **Changing employer.** MOHRE approves a transfer when the contract has ended, when a court finds the employer broke the contract, or when the employer agrees in writing \[[159 (2022)](#o159)\]. If your wage is cut or the employer breaks the contract, notify MOHRE; after two weeks without a fix you may leave, keep your rights, and transfer or go home \[[151 (2022)](#o151)\]. For a named recruit who moves in the first contract, the new employer repays part of the old employer's costs \[[151 (2022)](#o151)\].
2. **Cancelling your permit yourself.** You can ask MOHRE to cancel your work permit for any reason without the employer's consent; if the employer does not answer within 10 days it is processed \[[159 (2022)](#o159)\]. You must then change status or leave the UAE \[[151 (2022)](#o151)\]. Your residence visa is then cancelled through MOHRE or the Domestic Work Bundle \[[34](#o34)\].
3. **Absconding ("absence") reports.** The employer must report you within 5 days of an unexcused absence \[[35 (2022)](#o35), [156 (2024)](#o156)\]. Ten days in a row, or 15 days in a year, without an excuse ends the contract \[[35 (2022)](#o35)\]. MOHRE can cancel the report if you were absent under 10 days or lawfully, were at work or on leave, or went back to the recruiting office \[[159 (2022)](#o159)\]. A true report bars a new work permit for a year after you leave; so do drunkenness at work, assaulting the family, or posting photos or videos of the home \[[159 (2022)](#o159)\]. A false report costs the employer AED 5,000 \[[151 (2022)](#o151)\].
4. **Refunds between office and employer.** Under the classic package the office guarantees you to the employer for two years \[[155 (2024)](#o155)\]. During the six-month probation it must replace you or refund the employer if you prove incompetent or badly behaved, are medically unfit, quit or refuse to work, or the agreed conditions are not met; after probation, if you refuse to work or abscond \[[155 (2024)](#o155), [151 (2022)](#o151)\]. The refund is the full recruitment fee in your first month, the full recruitment and government fees if you are found medically unfit during probation, and otherwise (cost ÷ contract months) × months left; for example AED 7,000 of AED 12,000 after 10 of 24 months \[[155 (2024)](#o155), [151 (2022)](#o151), [156 (2024)](#o156)\]. It is due within 14 days of your return to the office or the absence report \[[155 (2024)](#o155), [151 (2022)](#o151)\]. Late refunds were 106 of 135 fines on offices in the first half of 2026 \[[N48](#n48)\]. The office must house and feed you if you come back to it for any reason, including being sent back, refusing work, illness or injury \[[155 (2024)](#o155), [35 (2022)](#o35)\].
5. **Going home.** If the employer ends the contract for a reason that is not your fault, it pays your ticket and all dues \[[35 (2022)](#o35)\]; dues are due within 10 days \[[35 (2022)](#o35)\]. Claims die 3 months after the job ends \[[35 (2022)](#o35)\].

> **Need to know**
> - Can a domestic worker move to a company (labour law) visa without leaving the UAE, and how?
> - Can a domestic worker sponsor a spouse or children, and does the AED 4,000 (or AED 3,000 plus housing) salary rule apply to them \[[142](#o142)\]?

### Scams

- **Unlicensed offices and online "maid" sales.** The UAE shut 230 social media accounts recruiting domestic workers without a licence in 2025; some sponsors and agents advertise "selling" workers online \[[N49](#n49)\].
- **The visit-visa job.** Some workers enter on tourist visas and are kept working illegally, then threatened with their status \[[N49](#n49)\]. A visit or tourist visa gives no right to work, and working on one brings fines and legal liability \[[6](#o6)\] (see Work visa: company worker, Step 1). To hire someone already in the UAE on a tourist visa, the household must first get a MOHRE domestic work permit for them \[[150](#o150), [6](#o6)\].
- **Big salary promises and contract switching.** Fake recruiters promise high pay; some workers find a lower wage on the UAE contract \[[N49](#n49), [N51 (2014)](#n51)\].
- **Fees.** An approved Bangladeshi agency or a UAE recruitment office that asks a woman domestic worker for money breaks the zero-cost rule and UAE law \[[153 (2024)](#o153), [35 (2022)](#o35)\]; so does a UAE employer \[[35 (2022)](#o35)\].

**Check it is real:**

1. **The Bangladeshi agency** must be one of the agencies the ministry approved for women domestic workers (21 in September 2024; see Step 3) \[[153 (2024)](#o153)\].
2. **The UAE office:** call MOHRE on 80060, choose your language, press 2 (domestic workers), choose to speak to a staff member and ask whether the office is licensed; or on mohre.gov.ae open Services > Enquiry services > Company Information and enter the licence number \[[N47 (2022)](#n47)\]. MOHRE also lists licensed offices under Services > Authorised Service Centres \[[154 (2023)](#o154)\].
3. **The visa:** have BMET check it (Step 4) \[[50](#o50)\].

**How to report:**

1. **A UAE recruitment office:** MOHRE on 600590000, its website or app, or the Labour Claims and Advisory Centre on 80084 \[[154 (2023)](#o154), [N48](#n48)\].
2. **A fake job or visa online, in Dubai:** the Dubai Police app or eCrime platform \[[N34](#n34)\].
3. **Abuse or trafficking of a woman:** DFWAC's free 24-hour helpline 800111 \[[161](#o161)\].
4. **A Bangladeshi agency:** a written complaint with evidence to BMET's Director General (free, 30 working days); Director (Employment) 01711111544, diremi@bmet.gov.bd \[[50](#o50)\].

## Tourist visa

**Status, November 2026:** tourist visas for Bangladeshis have been effectively stopped since mid-2024 \[[N5](#n5), [N11](#n11)\]. In October 2025 the immediate past secretary general of the Association of Travel Agents of Bangladesh (ATAB) said tourist and transit visas were "almost zero" \[[N19](#n19)\], and a director of the Tour Operators Association of Bangladesh (TOAB) who processed over 2,000 UAE tourist visas a year from 2021 to mid-2024 had not managed one in the year since \[[N19](#n19)\]. Even a 27-member official delegation to an Abu Dhabi congress got its visas only the night before the flight, after Foreign Ministry intervention \[[N19](#n19)\]. 

> **Need to know**
> - Does ICP's or GDRFA's system still reject an application at submission when the passport nationality is Bangladesh, for every visit visa type, as of November 2026?
> - Has any Bangladeshi-passport visit visa been issued in 2026, and through which channel (embassy courtesy visa, airline, family sponsor)?

A tourist visa lasts 30 or 60 days, single or multiple entry \[[163](#o163), [164](#o164)\]. A visitor cannot apply alone: a UAE tourism establishment (licensed travel agent, hotel or tour operator) or a UAE airline applies as sponsor \[[163](#o163), [42 (2022)](#o42), [41](#o41)\]. Airlines do it only for their own passengers \[[41](#o41)\]; travel agents and hotels only if the ticket is bought through them and the hotel booking is kept \[[41](#o41)\].

### Step 1: Choose who applies for you

1. **Pick one sponsor;** you cannot apply yourself \[[163](#o163), [83](#o83)\]:

   - **a UAE airline** (Emirates, Etihad, flydubai, Air Arabia), for its own passengers only \[[6](#o6), [41](#o41)\];
   - **a licensed UAE travel agent or hotel,** if you buy the ticket through it and keep the hotel booking; it files with ICP (the Federal Authority for Identity, Citizenship, Customs and Port Security, for Abu Dhabi and the northern emirates) or GDRFA Dubai (the General Directorate of Identity and Foreigners Affairs, for Dubai) \[[41](#o41), [163](#o163)\];
   - **a Bangladeshi travel agency** selling a package with a UAE tour operator \[[41](#o41)\].

2. **With Emirates, apply through "Manage your booking":**

   1. Buy an Emirates ticket into and out of Dubai on one booking reference (PNR), on Emirates flights only \[[8](#o8)\].
   2. Open the booking under "Manage your booking" and apply at least four international working days before arrival in Dubai \[[8](#o8)\].
   3. Pay the visa fee (Step 3) plus VFS Global's processing fee \[[8](#o8)\].

3. **With an agency, check it is genuine before paying or sending any document copies;** the UAE embassy can help verify \[[41](#o41)\].

   > **Need to know**
   > - How does the UAE Embassy in Dhaka verify a travel agency or UAE tour operator, and on what phone or email?

### Step 2: Prepare the papers

You'll need:

- a passport valid at least 6 months \[[80](#o80), [163](#o163)\];
- a photo and a passport copy \[[163](#o163), [80](#o80)\];
- a confirmed return or onward ticket \[[80](#o80), [163](#o163)\];
- UAE-valid health insurance \[[80](#o80), [163](#o163)\];
- for ICP, a tour programme statement \[[80](#o80)\];
- for Emirates, a confirmed hotel booking for the whole stay (or a tenancy contract in a family member's name) and a return Emirates ticket (number starting 176) whose dates match the hotel booking \[[8](#o8)\].

### Step 3: Pay

1. **Pay the sponsor.** The official fees are:

   - **GDRFA, single entry:** AED 200 (30 days) or AED 300 (60 days), plus 5% VAT (value added tax); no deposit \[[163](#o163)\];
   - **GDRFA, multiple entry:** AED 300 (30 days) or AED 500 (60 days), plus VAT \[[164](#o164)\];
   - **Emirates (VAT included):** US$90 for 30 days single entry up to US$231 for 60 days multiple entry; extension US$230 \[[8](#o8)\].

2. **Before paying an agency, compare its price** with the price list of Amer (GDRFA Dubai's visa service channel) at amer247.com \[[6](#o6)\].

3. **Wait** about 48 hours (GDRFA, ICP) or 2 to 4 working days (Emirates) \[[163](#o163), [83](#o83), [8](#o8)\]. **You get back** the visa by email, as an electronic copy \[[83](#o83)\].
4. **Enter within 60 days** of issue \[[80](#o80), [8](#o8)\].

**If it fails:** if ICP does not complete a service, ask for the issuance fee back with a bank certificate showing your account number and IBAN; application and smart-service fees are not refunded \[[165](#o165)\].

An agency travel blog lists Bangladeshi agency packages for a 30-day Dubai tourist visa at Tk 1 to 2 lakh, against about AED 340 (about Tk 11,600) for the visa online \[[N2](#n2), [2](#o2)\].

> **Need to know**
> - Can a refused UAE visit visa be appealed, or only re-applied for?
> - Are the Tk 1 to 2 lakh UAE tourist visa packages sold in Dhaka actually getting visas issued in 2026?

### Step 4: Check the visa is real

1. **Find which emirate issued it:** Dubai visas are checked with GDRFA, and visas for the other six emirates with ICP \[[95](#o95)\].
2. **Dubai visa:** open the GDRFA Status Inquiry page (smart.gdrfad.gov.ae/Public_Th/StatusInquiry_New.aspx) and enter the visa details \[[95](#o95)\].
3. **Abu Dhabi, Sharjah, Ajman, Umm Al Quwain, Ras Al Khaimah or Fujairah visa:** open ICP's File Validity page (smartservices.icp.gov.ae, "fileValidity"), leave "GCC Citizen" unticked, choose "File No." or "Passport Information" under "Search By" and "Visa" under "Select the Type" \[[95](#o95), [96](#o96)\]; to track an application, enter the application number, which starts with 010, under application tracking \[[83](#o83)\].
4. **Or phone:** ICP 600522222; GDRFA 800 5111 inside the UAE, +971 4 313 9999 from abroad \[[95](#o95)\].

> **Need to know**
> - With "Visa" selected, which fields does ICP's File Validity form ask for, and what do ICP's and GDRFA's pages show for a fake visa?

### Step 5: Board the flight

1. **On Air Arabia, get "OK to Board" on your booking;** passengers from Bangladesh with a copy of the visa or an e-visa need it \[[166](#o166)\]:

   1. The sponsor or a representative takes the original visa to the nearest Air Arabia office at least 24 hours before departure \[[166](#o166)\].
   2. They pay AED 15 to 20 \[[166](#o166)\].

   flydubai's OK to Board page lists advance approval only for flights from India and Pakistan and to Iraq, Bosnia, Syria and Libya, not from Bangladesh \[[N54](#n54)\].

> **Need to know**
> - Do Biman, US-Bangla and Emirates require OK to Board for Bangladeshi visit-visa passengers, and at what fee?

2. **At Dhaka or Chattogram airport, answer immigration police's questions** about your purpose and family; they stamp "offload" in the passports of visitors to Dubai they doubt \[[N55 (2021)](#n55)\]. In 2021 travel agencies charged Bangladeshis Tk 1.2 to 1.5 lakh for a UAE visit visa package covering visa processing, the ticket, a promised job and "airport contract" money; agencies said the Tk 30,000 airport-contract charge went to immigration police \[[N55 (2021)](#n55)\].

> **Need to know**
> - Does the "airport contract" for UAE visit-visa travellers still operate in 2026, and at what price?

3. **At the UAE airport, do the eye scan at passport control if asked** \[[167](#o167)\]. For a visa problem in Dubai, GDRFA's visa office in Terminal 3 arrivals, near Entrance 1, is open 24 hours \[[167](#o167)\].

> **Need to know**
> - Do UAE immigration officers ask Bangladeshi visitors to show a minimum amount of cash or a hotel booking on arrival?

### Step 6: Extend or leave

1. **Extend before it expires, if you need to,** up to 120 days in total \[[168](#o168)\].

   You'll need \[[168](#o168)\]:

   - your passport;
   - a personal photo;
   - the same documents as when the visa was issued.

   1. For an ICP visa, open ICP's "Visa Extension" service at [smartservices.icp.gov.ae](https://smartservices.icp.gov.ae/echannels/web/client/default.html#/login) and log in with UAE Pass, the UAE's digital ID \[[168](#o168)\].
   2. Upload the documents and pay AED 100 application plus AED 500 extension \[[168](#o168)\].

   > **Need to know**
   > - Does the visitor or the sponsoring agency, hotel or airline file a tourist visa extension, and which GDRFA service extends a Dubai-issued one?

2. **Leave when the visa expires** \[[125 (2021)](#o125)\].
3. **If you overstay,** the fine is AED 50 a day \[[80](#o80)\]; check it on GDRFA's fines inquiry service and pay through ICP or GDRFA \[[169](#o169)\].

### 5-year multiple-entry tourist visa

This self-sponsored visa is open to all nationalities, including Bangladeshis: valid 5 years, up to 90 days each visit, extendable by 90 days, no more than 180 days a year \[[41](#o41), [170](#o170), [N56](#n56)\]. The year counts from the first entry, and unused days don't carry over \[[170](#o170)\].

> **Need to know**
> - The ICP card also says the holder must not exceed 90 days a year \[[170](#o170)\]: is the yearly limit 90 or 180 days?
> - Has any Bangladeshi-passport holder been granted the 5-year visa since mid-2024?

You'll need:

- a bank statement showing at least USD 4,000 (about Tk 5 lakh) throughout the 6 months before applying \[[170](#o170), [N56](#n56)\];
- UAE-issued health insurance valid 180 days \[[170](#o170)\];
- a return ticket and proof of where you'll stay \[[170](#o170)\];
- passport valid 6 months and a photo \[[170](#o170)\].

1. **Apply yourself** online on ICP's smart services ("Issuance of a Long-Term Multiple-Entry Tourist Visa – 5 Years") or GDRFA's ("Issuing a multi-entry tourist visa (5 years)"), 24 hours a day, or at a GDRFA Customer Happiness Centre or Amer centre (Dubai's licensed government-service centres) \[[170](#o170), [171](#o171), [172](#o172)\]. Families use ICP's family-group version; the head of family must enter with them \[[173](#o173)\].
2. **Pay** AED 100 application, AED 500 issuance, AED 100 smart-service fee and a refundable AED 3,000 guarantee plus AED 20; GDRFA's total is AED 3,713 (about Tk 1.26 lakh) \[[170](#o170), [171](#o171)\].
3. **Enter within 60 days** of issue; after that the visa is multiple-entry \[[170](#o170)\].
4. **Track your days** by requesting a travel report of entries and exits; an overstay is fined at the departure port and the AED 3,000 guarantee is forfeited \[[171](#o171)\].

### Bangladeshis living in another Gulf country

A Bangladeshi resident of a GCC (Gulf Cooperation Council) country applies online for an entry permit before travelling \[[41](#o41)\]. The residence must be valid at least a year from arrival and the profession must be on the UAE's approved list \[[41](#o41)\]. The permit is valid 30 days from issue, allows 30 days' stay and can be extended once for 30 days \[[41](#o41)\]. GDRFA charges AED 100 application, AED 100 issuance and AED 52.5 service fee, decided in 48 hours; you upload a photo, passport copy and your residence permit showing your profession \[[174](#o174)\].

> **Need to know**
> - Which professions are on the approved list for the GCC-resident entry permit?

### Scams

**Done to travellers.** In September 2025 a visa-agency website, uaevisaonline.com, posted that the UAE would ban tourist and work visas for Bangladesh and eight other countries from January 2026; Bangladeshi media repeated it \[[N57](#n57)\]. The Bangladesh Embassy in Abu Dhabi said the claim was false, that the UAE had made no such announcement, that the site's contacts were in the UK, US and India, that its Dubai head-office address did not exist, and that most of its customers complained of being cheated \[[16](#o16)\]. A visa itself can be checked on GDRFA's website if it was issued in Dubai, or on ICP's eChannels platform if it was issued in another emirate (see Tourist visa, Step 4) \[[6](#o6)\].

On 19 June 2026 GDRFA Dubai said it had acted against entities selling unauthorised visa and residence services on social media, and told people to use only official channels and to check the source before sharing personal information or paying \[[175](#o175)\].   Tourist entry permits can be issued by UAE airlines (Emirates, Etihad, flydubai, Air Arabia), licensed hotels and licensed travel agencies \[[6](#o6)\].

**Tried by travellers.** A false statement to get a visa carries up to 6 months' jail and/or a fine of AED 5,000 to 10,000, and possible deportation \[[125 (2021)](#o125)\]. Forging a visa, or knowingly using a forged one, carries up to 10 years' jail and mandatory deportation \[[125 (2021)](#o125)\].

If someone sold you a fake visa or a "job on a visit visa", or holds you in the UAE, report it to MOHRE (+971 6 802 7666, ask@mohre.gov.ae) and, in Dubai, to Dubai Police on 901, its app or eCrime platform (see Work visa: company worker, Step 5) \[[6](#o6), [N34](#n34)\].

### Don't work

A visit or tourist visa gives no right to work; working on one brings fines and legal liability \[[6](#o6)\]. The law forbids any work except as UAE legislation allows \[[125 (2021)](#o125)\]. Anyone who employs or houses a person in breach of the residence law is fined AED 50,000 per person, up to AED 5 million, and jailed on a repeat; the worker is deported \[[125 (2021)](#o125)\]. Using or helping to use a visa for a purpose other than the one it was issued for carries jail or a fine of at least AED 10,000 per person, and deportation \[[125 (2021)](#o125)\]. Under the Labour Law, employing a worker without a work permit costs the employer AED 100,000 to 1,000,000 \[[10 (2021)](#o10)\].

## Job-seeker visit visa

**Status, November 2026:** visit visas for Bangladeshis are restricted (see Background, "Is it open now") \[[N5](#n5)\]; only skilled and high-profile categories were reported open in January 2026 \[[N28](#n28)\].

This visa lets a person enter once to look for work without a UAE sponsor, for 60, 90 or 120 days \[[176](#o176), [177](#o177)\]. It is only for:

- a skilled worker at MOHRE (Ministry of Human Resources and Emiratisation) skill level 1, 2 or 3; or
- a graduate of one of the world's top 500 universities (Ministry of Education ranking) who graduated within the last 2 years;

and in both cases with at least a bachelor's degree \[[176](#o176), [177](#o177), [42 (2022)](#o42)\].

> **Need to know**
> - How does a Bangladeshi abroad get MOHRE's certificate of skill level, which ICP lists as a document?

### Step 1: Prepare

You'll need:

- a passport valid 6 months and a colour photo \[[176](#o176), [80](#o80)\];
- the attested bachelor's degree certificate \[[176](#o176), [177](#o177)\];
- MOHRE's certificate confirming your skilled-worker level, or, for a top-500 graduate, the Ministry of Education's certificate of the university's ranking \[[177](#o177)\];
- UAE-valid health insurance \[[80](#o80)\].

### Step 2: Apply

1. **Apply online yourself;** no UAE sponsor is needed \[[176](#o176)\]:

   - **ICP:** open [smartservices.icp.gov.ae](https://smartservices.icp.gov.ae/echannels/web/client/default.html#/login) or the ICP app, log in with UAE Pass (the UAE's digital ID) and choose "Issuance of a Job Exploration Visa" for 60, 90 or 120 days, single entry \[[177](#o177), [176](#o176), [80](#o80)\];
   - **GDRFA (Dubai):** open [smart.gdrfad.gov.ae](https://smart.gdrfad.gov.ae/SmartChannels_Th/Login.aspx?Lang=ar-AE), log in with UAE Pass or a username, search for "Issuing a visit visa to explore job opportunities", fill in the application and upload the papers \[[178](#o178), [176](#o176), [172](#o172)\].

   > **Need to know**
   > - Can a Bangladeshi outside the UAE open a UAE Pass or a GDRFA username to apply?

2. **Or in Dubai at a counter:** at a GDRFA Customer Happiness Centre or an Amer centre, take the automated turn ticket, hand the application and papers to the employee and pay \[[172](#o172)\].
3. **Pay:** ICP AED 100 application, AED 100 issuance, AED 100 smart-service fee and a refundable AED 1,000 guarantee \[[177](#o177)\]; GDRFA AED 200 (60 days), AED 300 (90) or AED 400 (120), plus 5% VAT and the AED 1,000 security \[[172](#o172)\].
4. **Wait about 48 hours** \[[172](#o172)\]. Enter within 60 days of issue \[[177](#o177)\].

### Step 3: Extend, then leave or switch

1. **Extend if you need to,** up to 180 days in total, through ICP's "Visa Extension" service (see Tourist visa, Step 6), for AED 100 plus AED 500 \[[168](#o168)\].
2. **Leave, then claim the AED 1,000 back.** It is refunded only if the visa is cancelled after you leave and you have no violations \[[177](#o177)\]. ICP refunds guarantees through its online "Refund of fees for approved applications" service on smartservices.icp.gov.ae \[[165](#o165)\].

   > **Need to know**
   > - Who files the guarantee refund once the visitor has left, and how long does it take?

3. **If you find a job, get a MOHRE offer letter;** the employer then sends an electronic entry permit for employment (see Work visa: company worker) \[[6](#o6)\]. For Bangladeshis the visit-to-work switch inside the UAE has been closed for years \[[N28](#n28)\].

### Scams

**Done to job-seekers.** A genuine job comes with a MOHRE offer letter, which you can check by its reference number at inquiry.mohre.gov.ae \[[6](#o6)\]. The employer, not the worker, pays recruitment costs \[[6](#o6)\]. Check the company on the National Economic Register (ner.economy.ae) \[[6](#o6)\]. MOHRE answers recruitment questions on +971 6 802 7666 and ask@mohre.gov.ae \[[6](#o6)\].

> **Need to know**
> - Are Bangladeshi agents selling "job-seeker visas" to people without a degree, and at what price?

**Tried by job-seekers.** A fake degree or false statement to qualify is a crime (see Tourist visa, Scams) \[[125 (2021)](#o125)\].

### Don't work

The job-seeker visa is for looking, not working; the penalties in Tourist visa, Don't work apply \[[6](#o6), [125 (2021)](#o125), [10 (2021)](#o10)\].

## Family visit visa

**Status, November 2026:** visit visas for Bangladeshis were restricted from July 2024 and a ministry official said none were issued in 2025 \[[N5](#n5)\].

> **Need to know**
> - Are UAE residents (including Golden Visa holders) able to get visit visas for Bangladeshi parents, spouses or children in 2026?

A UAE resident can sponsor a relative's or friend's visit for 30, 60 or 90 days, single or multiple entry \[[80](#o80), [N58](#n58), [178](#o178)\]. The sponsor must earn at least \[[80](#o80), [179](#o179)\]:

- AED 4,000 a month for a first-degree relative: father, mother, spouse, son, daughter;
- AED 8,000 for a second- or third-degree relative: sibling, grandparent, grandchild, uncle, aunt, cousin;
- AED 15,000 for a friend \[[80](#o80)\].

In humanitarian cases ICP's Director General of Identity and Foreigners Affairs can waive the income or kinship rule \[[179](#o179)\].

### Step 1: The visitor sends papers to the sponsor

You'll need:

- a passport valid at least 6 months and a recent photo \[[80](#o80), [N58](#n58)\];
- the attested marriage, birth or family certificate proving the relationship \[[N58](#n58), [80](#o80)\];
- a return ticket and UAE-valid health insurance \[[80](#o80)\].

### Step 2: The sponsor applies in the UAE

You'll need (the sponsor): a valid UAE residence visa and Emirates ID, an active sponsor file, and proof of income (salary certificate from a government or free-zone employer, employment contract for a private company, or partnership contract), plus proof of accommodation \[[N58](#n58), [80](#o80), [178](#o178)\].

1. **Apply** through one of these:

   - **Abu Dhabi and the northern emirates (ICP):** open [smartservices.icp.gov.ae](https://smartservices.icp.gov.ae/echannels/web/client/default.html#/login) or the ICP Smart App, log in with UAE Pass, choose "Issuance of a Visa" and the category "Visitor – For visiting relatives, friends", fill in the form and upload the papers \[[80](#o80)\].
   - **Dubai (GDRFA), online:** open [smart.gdrfad.gov.ae](https://smart.gdrfad.gov.ae/SmartChannels_Th/Login.aspx?Lang=ar-AE) or the GDRFA app, log in with UAE Pass or a username, search for the relative or friend visit visa, fill in the application and pay \[[178](#o178), [N58](#n58)\].
   - **Dubai, at a counter:** at an Amer centre or a Customer Happiness Centre, take the automatic queue ticket, hand the application and papers to the employee and pay \[[178](#o178), [N58](#n58)\].
   - **Emirates passengers:** at any Emirates ticketing office in the UAE \[[8](#o8)\].
2. **Pay:**
   - ICP: AED 100 application, AED 100 per month (single entry) or AED 200 per month (multiple entry), AED 100 smart-service fee, and a refundable guarantee of AED 1,000 (single) or AED 2,000 (multiple) plus AED 20 \[[80](#o80)\];
   - GDRFA: AED 200 (30 days), AED 300 (60) or AED 400 (90) single entry, or AED 300, 500 or 700 multiple entry, plus 5% VAT, insurance AED 40 to 90, and a refundable AED 1,000 (single) or AED 2,000 (multiple) deposit \[[178](#o178), [N58](#n58)\].
3. **Wait about 48 hours**; the visa is emailed \[[83](#o83)\]. Track an ICP application at smartservices.icp.gov.ae under application tracking, with the application number starting 010 \[[83](#o83)\].
4. **Send the visa to the visitor,** who must enter within 60 days \[[80](#o80)\].

### Step 3: Stay, extend, leave

1. **Extend before the visa runs out, if needed,** through ICP's "Visa Extension" service (see Tourist visa, Step 6), on the same documents, for AED 100 plus AED 500 \[[168](#o168)\].
2. **The visitor leaves** when the visa expires \[[125 (2021)](#o125)\].
3. **The sponsor claims the guarantee back** with a refund request after the visa is cancelled or the visitor exits, if there was no violation \[[80](#o80)\]; ICP's online service is "Refund of fees for approved applications" \[[165](#o165)\].

> **Need to know**
> - ICP's two service cards give a different maximum total stay for a relative's visit (120 days \[[80](#o80)\] and 180 days \[[168](#o168)\]): which applies?

### Scams

**Done to families.** GDRFA warned in June 2026 that entities promoting unauthorised visa and residence services online could expose people to exploitation \[[175](#o175)\]. The visa is applied for by the UAE-resident sponsor, who must have an active sponsor file, a valid residence visa and Emirates ID, and proof of income \[[80](#o80), [N58](#n58)\].

**Tried by families.** A false relationship certificate or salary paper is a false statement or forgery (see Tourist visa, Scams) \[[125 (2021)](#o125)\]; the sponsor faces AED 50,000 per person for harbouring someone who breaks the residence law \[[125 (2021)](#o125)\].

### Don't work

A visit visa holder may not work (see Tourist visa, Don't work) \[[6](#o6)\]. An overstay costs AED 50 a day, and the sponsor loses the guarantee \[[80](#o80)\].

## Family residence (spouse, children and parents)

**Status for Bangladeshis (Nov 2026).** The rules below are the law for every nationality, but since July 2024 Bangladeshis have met restrictions and long stalls on new family visas, as on student, tourist and employment visas and company-to-company transfers; Bangladesh's Expatriates' Welfare Minister said so on 17 August 2026 \[[N43](#n43)\]. Reporting in January 2026 said only "skill visas" and "high-profile visas" were being issued to Bangladeshis \[[N28](#n28)\].

> **Need to know**
> - Are GDRFA and ICP approving new family residence applications by Bangladeshi sponsors in November 2026, and for which salary or job levels?
> - Are renewals of existing Bangladeshi family visas going through normally?

A resident worker (man or woman) can sponsor a spouse, unmarried daughters of any age, sons under 25 and children with special needs \[[142](#o142)\]. The family visa runs for the same period as the sponsor's and can never be longer \[[82 (2024)](#o82)\]. If the sponsor's visa is cancelled, the family's visas must be cancelled too; the family then has 6 months to get a new residence permit, and a sponsor who neither renews nor cancels can be fined \[[142](#o142)\]. A residence permit becomes void after more than 180 consecutive days outside the UAE \[[42 (2022)](#o42)\].

**Who qualifies to sponsor** \[[142](#o142), [82 (2024)](#o82), [42 (2022)](#o42)\]:

- a valid UAE residence permit;
- salary of at least AED 4,000 a month, or AED 3,000 plus housing given by the employer;
- housing big enough for the family, owned or rented;
- proof of the relationship.

Dubai has a separate humanitarian route for a worker earning under AED 4,000 whose family's residence expired or was cancelled inside the UAE; it needs the attested marriage and birth certificates, 3 months' bank statements and an attested tenancy contract, and a committee decides each case \[[180](#o180)\].

### Step 1: Get the marriage and birth certificates attested

The UAE accepts a Bangladeshi certificate only after three attestations: Bangladesh's Ministry of Foreign Affairs (MoFA), then the UAE Embassy in Bangladesh, then the UAE Ministry of Foreign Affairs (UAE MOFA) \[[N59](#n59)\].

1. **Notarise the certificates.** MoFA works from a notarised scan of the nikahnama or marriage documents issued by the nikah registrar, and of the birth certificate issued by the city corporation, municipality or union parishad \[[92](#o92)\]. A hospital birth certificate must first be attested by the Civil Surgeon or the Health Ministry \[[92](#o92)\].
2. **Apply to MoFA on mygov.bd.** Register, choose the option for the document type (marriage certificate or birth certificate), fill in the form and upload the scan as JPEG, JPG or PNG \[[92](#o92)\]. It is free and takes 5 working days; an SMS tells you when the attested copy is ready to download from your mygov account \[[92](#o92)\]. Questions: MoFA consular section, 024152830, asconsular2@mofa.gov.bd \[[92](#o92)\].
3. **Apply for the UAE attestations online.** Make a UAE Pass account, log in at mofa.gov.ae and choose Services > Services for Individuals > Document Attestation > UAE Embassy and Consulate abroad > Bangladesh > mission "VFS delivery service" > "Issued from Outside UAE" \[[N59](#n59)\]. Pay AED 300 per personal document on the portal (AED 150 Embassy + AED 150 UAE MOFA) plus the service fee of VFS Global, the private company that collects and returns documents for the UAE Embassy in Bangladesh \[[N59](#n59)\]. If the application is rejected only the Embassy fee comes back \[[N59](#n59)\].
4. **Hand over the originals.** A VFS courier collects them within 48-72 hours, or you drop them at the VFS UAE Attestation Centre; the Embassy and UAE MOFA attest digitally in 2-3 working days and nothing more is needed inside the UAE \[[N59](#n59)\]. VFS call centre: +8809606 777 919 \[[N59](#n59)\].

**Check it is real:** the original is not stamped; download the attested document at verify.mofa.gov.ae with the reference number and issue date \[[N59](#n59), [181](#o181)\].

> **Need to know**
> - What is VFS Global's service fee in Taka for one UAE attestation in Dhaka?
> - Where are VFS's UAE Attestation Centres in Bangladesh?
> - Does ICP or GDRFA also ask for an Arabic translation of a Bangladeshi nikahnama?

### Step 2: Prepare the sponsor's papers and housing

You'll need \[[82 (2024)](#o82), [182](#o182), [42 (2022)](#o42)\]:

- a passport copy (valid 6 months) and photo for each family member, and the sponsor's valid residence permit and Emirates ID (the national identity card for residents);
- the employment contract, or a salary certificate if the employer is government, semi-government or free zone;
- an employment certificate showing monthly income and housing;
- a water or electricity bill;
- the attested marriage certificate and children's birth certificates;
- health insurance for each family member.

In Dubai the sponsor must buy health insurance for resident dependants; in Abu Dhabi the employer or sponsor covers one spouse and 3 children under 18 \[[31](#o31)\]. A partner sponsoring family in Dubai needs a share of at least AED 48,000 \[[182](#o182)\]. A wife sponsoring needs a no-objection from the father \[[182](#o182)\].

In Dubai, a rental is registered in Ejari (Dubai Land Department's tenancy register) with the unified tenancy contract and the tenant's Emirates ID, for AED 177.75 to AED 220 in total \[[183](#o183)\].

> **Need to know**
> - Does GDRFA still require an Ejari (Dubai) or Tawtheeq (Abu Dhabi) tenancy contract to sponsor a spouse and children, or only for parents?
> - Is a bed-space or shared-flat tenancy accepted for a family visa?

### Step 3: Apply for the entry permit, then the residence

1. **Apply for the family entry permit** in the sponsor's emirate: Dubai residents with GDRFA (General Directorate of Identity and Foreigners Affairs, Dubai) online with UAE Pass, at a GDRFA Customer Happiness Centre or at an Amer centre (Dubai's licensed typing centres); other emirates with ICP (Federal Authority for Identity, Citizenship, Customs and Port Security) on smartservices.icp.gov.ae or the ICP app, which needs UAE Pass \[[182](#o182), [82 (2024)](#o82)\].
2. **Pay.** Dubai: AED 200 plus 5% VAT; if the family member is already in the UAE, add AED 10 knowledge fee, AED 10 innovation fee and AED 500 \[[182](#o182)\]. ICP's fees for the residence permit: AED 100 application, AED 100 per year of residence, AED 100 smart service fee, AED 20 deposit fee, and AED 500 for a change of status inside the UAE \[[82 (2024)](#o82)\]. GDRFA quotes 48 hours for the entry permit; ICP quotes 2 days for the residence permit \[[182](#o182), [82 (2024)](#o82)\].
3. **Enter within the permit's validity and finish within 60 days.** The permit allows 60 days from entry to complete the residence; after that overstay costs AED 50 a day \[[182](#o182), [82 (2024)](#o82)\].
4. **Medical and Emirates ID.** Family members aged 18 or over must pass the medical fitness test at an approved centre in the UAE \[[142](#o142), [184](#o184)\]. Then apply for the residence permit and Emirates ID: Dubai charges AED 200 plus AED 10, AED 10, AED 500 if inside the UAE, and AED 20 delivery, rising by AED 100 a year for residence over two years \[[184](#o184)\]. If nothing is missing GDRFA emails the approval \[[184](#o184)\].

**Check it is real:** check an Abu Dhabi or Northern Emirates visa on ICP's File Validity page and a Dubai visa on GDRFA's Status Inquiry page \[[95](#o95)\]. ICP call centre: 600522222 \[[82 (2024)](#o82)\].

> **Need to know**
> - Is there a separate refundable deposit for a spouse or child's residence in any emirate in 2026?
> - Can a refused family residence application be appealed, and where?

### Parents

In Dubai, parents (and minor brothers and sisters who depend on the worker, or the spouse's parents and minor children) come under GDRFA's humanitarian-case residence: one year, renewable, decided case by case by a committee \[[180](#o180)\]. You'll need the parent's passport copy, a salary certificate or contract showing at least AED 10,000 a month (or partnership papers), an attested tenancy contract big enough for the family, and 3 months' bank statements \[[180](#o180)\]. On approval GDRFA takes a guarantee of AED 5,000 per request, up to AED 15,000 in total \[[180](#o180)\]. Fees are AED 200 plus the same extras as above; it takes about 14 days, through the GDRFA app or website, Amer centres or the Humanitarian Cases Section at Festival Centre \[[180](#o180)\]. The grace period after it ends is 60 days \[[180](#o180)\]. Children aged 25 or over can be sponsored with certified proof of continuing studies or a certified university graduation certificate (attested by the UAE Embassy and the Ministry of Foreign Affairs in Dubai if issued abroad) \[[180](#o180)\]. A resident's widow or divorcee inside the UAE whose husband was her sponsor can sponsor herself within 6 months of the death or divorce, and her children if she has legal custody \[[180](#o180)\]. Green visa holders may sponsor first-degree relatives, including parents \[[142](#o142), [42 (2022)](#o42)\].

> **Need to know**
> - What do ICP (Abu Dhabi and Northern Emirates) require to sponsor a resident's parents: salary threshold and deposit?

### Can a family member work?

Yes, but only with a work permit. A foreigner may not work, paid or unpaid, without one \[[42 (2022)](#o42)\]. The employer applies to MOHRE (Ministry of Human Resources and Emiratisation) for a "Dependents Sponsored by Family Members" work permit; the family member keeps the family visa \[[185](#o185)\]. Conditions: aged 18 or over, no other active permit, valid residence under the relative; all skill levels; no quota needed; valid 2 years; full-time, part-time, temporary, flexible, remote or job-sharing contracts \[[185](#o185)\]. Fees are AED 50 to apply and AED 250 to issue, free online apart from these, with typing-centre commission capped at AED 72; MOHRE takes 2 working days \[[185](#o185)\]. In the DMCC (Dubai Multi Commodities Centre) free zone the employer instead applies for a one-year identity card, counted in its visa quota \[[186](#o186)\].

## Business visit

**Status, November 2026:** visas for Bangladeshi delegations are slow: in October 2025 a Bangladeshi delegation to a conference in Abu Dhabi got its visas only on the night before its flight, after intervention by the Ministry of Foreign Affairs \[[N19](#n19)\]; group visas for business delegations were said to be speeded up in May 2025 \[[N60](#n60)\].

### Explore business or investment

A single-entry visa for 60, 90 or 120 days, with no UAE sponsor, for people who are financially solvent, talented or highly qualified and work in the intended business at home \[[187](#o187)\]. Since September 2025 the applicant must show financial capability for the activity, run it through a company abroad, or practise the profession \[[179](#o179)\].

You'll need:

- a passport valid 6 months and a photo \[[188](#o188), [80](#o80)\];
- initial approval from the UAE authority for the planned business \[[188](#o188)\];
- a stamped bank statement for at least 6 months \[[188](#o188)\];
- a bachelor's degree certificate (ICP) or proof of investment (GDRFA) \[[188](#o188), [189](#o189)\].

1. **Apply** online, yourself:

   - **ICP:** open [smartservices.icp.gov.ae](https://smartservices.icp.gov.ae/echannels/web/client/default.html#/login), log in with UAE Pass and choose "Issuance of a Visa to Explore Business Establishment Opportunities" \[[188](#o188)\];
   - **GDRFA (Dubai):** open [smart.gdrfad.gov.ae](https://smart.gdrfad.gov.ae/SmartChannels_Th/Login.aspx?Lang=ar-AE), log in with UAE Pass or a username, search for "Issuing a visit visa for the purpose of exploring business opportunities" and fill in the application \[[178](#o178), [189](#o189)\].

2. **Pay** ICP AED 300 in fees plus a refundable AED 1,000 guarantee, or GDRFA AED 200 to 400 plus VAT and AED 1,000 \[[188](#o188), [189](#o189)\].
3. **Extend** if needed, up to 180 days in total, through ICP's "Visa Extension" service (see Tourist visa, Step 6), for AED 100 plus AED 500 \[[168](#o168)\].

### Events, conferences and training

Since September 2025 there is an Events Visit Visa for exhibitions, conferences, seminars, festivals and similar; a UAE public or private host applies, with a letter giving the event and its dates \[[179](#o179)\]. For study or training, a licensed UAE university, institute, research body or a government or private trainer applies with a letter stating the programme and its length; GDRFA grants 30, 60 or 180 days for AED 200 to 400 plus VAT and an AED 1,000 deposit \[[80](#o80), [190](#o190)\]. The same September 2025 decision added visit visas for AI specialists, entertainment visits and cruise or leisure-boat tourism \[[179](#o179)\].

> **Need to know**
> - Do UAE exhibitions (for example Gulfood) currently get Events Visit Visas approved for Bangladeshi participants?
> - Is ICP's truck-driver visit visa (single or multiple trips, sponsored by a shipping or cargo company, revised in September 2025) issued to Bangladeshi drivers, and on what terms?

### Scams

**Done to business travellers.** Check any business or exhibition visa before paying or travelling: ICP's File Validity page for Abu Dhabi and the Northern Emirates, GDRFA's Status Inquiry page for Dubai (see Tourist visa, Step 4) \[[95](#o95)\].

> **Need to know**
> - What fake "business" or "exhibition" visa schemes target Bangladeshis going to the UAE?

### Don't work

Meetings and exploring are allowed; work is not (see Tourist visa, Don't work) \[[6](#o6), [125 (2021)](#o125)\].

## Medical visit

**Status, November 2026:** visit visas for Bangladeshis are restricted \[[N5](#n5)\]; the UAE ambassador promised flexibility for humanitarian cases raised by Bangladeshi authorities (May 2025) \[[N61](#n61)\].

A licensed UAE hospital applies for the patient, single or multiple entry; in Dubai the visa is for 90 or 180 days \[[191](#o191)\].

### Step 1: Get the hospital to sponsor you

You'll need:

- a passport copy (valid 6 months) and a photo \[[191](#o191), [80](#o80)\];
- a medical report \[[191](#o191), [80](#o80)\];
- health insurance \[[191](#o191)\].

1. **Send the papers above to a licensed UAE hospital** and ask it to sponsor you \[[191](#o191)\].
2. **The hospital writes the letter** stating the reasons for the visit, to go with an approved medical report \[[80](#o80), [192](#o192)\].
3. **Pay the financial security deposit** (amounts in Step 2) \[[191](#o191), [192](#o192)\].

### Step 2: The hospital applies

- **GDRFA (Dubai):** the hospital logs in at [smart.gdrfad.gov.ae](https://smart.gdrfad.gov.ae/SmartChannels_Th/Login.aspx?Lang=ar-AE) with UAE Pass or a username, searches for the medical visa service, fills in the application and pays AED 200 (90 days) or AED 500 (180 days) single entry plus VAT; deposit AED 1,000 single, AED 2,000 multiple \[[178](#o178), [192](#o192)\].
- **ICP (other emirates):** the hospital applies at [smartservices.icp.gov.ae](https://smartservices.icp.gov.ae/echannels/web/client/default.html#/login) through "Issuance of a Visa", category licensed healthcare facilities for treatment or patient companions \[[80](#o80)\].

### Step 3: Companions and extensions

1. **For a companion,** the hospital applies for the same type of visa with its letter and a copy of the patient's visa; ICP also asks for the health authority's approval letter; the companion must enter with the patient \[[80](#o80), [191](#o191)\].
2. **Extend the patient's visa** if treatment continues, up to 180 days in total, through the authority that issued it (ICP's "Visa Extension" service outside Dubai; see Tourist visa, Step 6) \[[168](#o168)\]. The companion's is extended only once, on a certified medical report that treatment must continue, and only if the patient's is \[[191](#o191)\].

> **Need to know**
> - Which UAE hospitals currently sponsor Bangladeshi patients, and how long do they take?

### Scams

> **Need to know**
> - What medical-visa scams (fake hospital letters, "treatment" visas sold as job visas) target Bangladeshis for the UAE?

### Don't work

No work on a medical visa (see Tourist visa, Don't work) \[[6](#o6)\].

## Mission work visa (temporary work)

**Status, November 2026:** work visas for Bangladeshis are restricted except skilled categories (see Work visa: company worker) \[[N5](#n5), [N28](#n28)\].

A UAE company registered with MOHRE gets a Mission Work Permit to bring a worker from abroad for a temporary job or time-bound project \[[32](#o32), [193](#o193)\]. It lasts three months, renewable once \[[193](#o193)\]. All skill levels and professions can apply, if the worker is 18 or older and has no active work permit \[[193](#o193)\]. You cannot apply yourself: the company applies, and ICP then issues the entry visa (work mission category) \[[193](#o193), [80](#o80)\].

### Step 1: The company applies to MOHRE

You'll need (sent to the company):

- a colour photo with white background \[[193](#o193)\];
- a passport copy valid 6 months \[[193](#o193)\];
- MOHRE's job offer form, signed by you and the employer \[[193](#o193)\];
- certificates for your skill level: bachelor's for levels 1 and 2, diploma for 3 and 4, high school for 5, none for 6 to 9 \[[193](#o193)\];
- a professional licence if the job needs one \[[193](#o193)\].

1. The company applies on the MOHRE website, app or a service centre, which needs an electronic quota \[[193](#o193)\].
2. MOHRE decides in 2 working days; the company pays the AED 50 federal fee (a centre's commission is capped at AED 72) and a bank guarantee or insurance \[[193](#o193)\].
3. **Check it:** the application status is on mohre.gov.ae (Inquiry), the MOHRE app, or 600590000 \[[193](#o193)\].

### Step 2: The entry visa

ICP's work-mission visa needs an employer certificate, a temporary job contract or letter of purpose, a medical fitness certificate for work, a ticket, and an attested good-conduct certificate for some categories \[[80](#o80), [42 (2022)](#o42)\]. It can be extended once, with MOHRE's approval \[[168](#o168)\].

> **Need to know**
> - Does a mission-visa worker need BMET emigration clearance, like a worker with a full work visa?
> - Are mission visas being issued to Bangladeshis in 2026?

### Scams

**Done to workers.** A "mission visa" sold as a two-year job is a 3-month permit, renewable once \[[193](#o193)\]. Check the MOHRE offer by its number at inquiry.mohre.gov.ae \[[6](#o6)\].

### Don't work

Work only for the company named on the permit; working for others breaks the permit and the residence law (see Tourist visa, Don't work) \[[125 (2021)](#o125), [10 (2021)](#o10)\].

## Transit

**Status, November 2026:** transit visas for Bangladeshis were reported restricted and "almost zero" in October 2025 \[[N19](#n19)\].

Leaving the airport during a connection needs a pre-arranged transit visa for anyone not eligible for visa on arrival, which includes Bangladeshis \[[194](#o194), [7](#o7), [8](#o8), [39](#o39)\]. It is for 48 or 96 hours, single entry, not extendable \[[194](#o194)\]. It needs a passport valid for the UAE and the next country and an onward ticket \[[42 (2022)](#o42)\].

1. **Apply** through the UAE airline you fly, a travel agency or an airport hotel \[[194](#o194)\]. Emirates issues it for travel on Emirates tickets out of Dubai, online in "Manage your booking" or at the Emirates Commercial Visa office, Dubai airport Terminal 3 \[[8](#o8)\].
2. **Pay** Emirates US$10 (48 hours) or US$49 (96 hours); transit fees are never refunded \[[8](#o8)\].

> **Need to know**
> - Can a Bangladeshi change planes in Dubai without a visa if they stay airside?

### Scams

> **Need to know**
> - Are transit visas used to send Bangladeshis to the UAE for work, and at what price?

### Don't work

A transit visa allows no work or overstay (see Tourist visa, Don't work) \[[6](#o6), [80](#o80)\].

## Student visa

**Status for Bangladeshis (Nov 2026):** new student visas for Bangladeshis are among those stalled since July 2024, the Expatriates' Welfare Minister said on 17 August 2026 \[[N43](#n43)\].

> **Need to know**
> - Are UAE universities currently getting student visas approved for applicants from Bangladesh, and which universities?

A student stays either on a resident parent's sponsorship or on the university's \[[195](#o195)\]. A son over 25 can stay on a parent's visa with proof he is in higher education, and a daughter can be sponsored to study at any age until she marries \[[195](#o195)\]. The university is the sponsor for a university visa, and it must be licensed and approved by the Ministry of Education or the emirate's education authority \[[42 (2022)](#o42)\]. Students' families may stay if they can afford suitable housing \[[195](#o195)\].

### Step 1: Check the university

1. **Check it is licensed** on caa.ae > HE Institutions (the Commission for Academic Accreditation list), filter "Active"; it shows each institution's emirate and status (Active, Inactive, Currently on Probation) \[[196](#o196)\].
2. **In the DMCC free zone in Dubai,** a university student visa needs a letter from KHDA (Dubai's Knowledge and Human Development Authority), and an institute's student visa is a 90-day entry permit, extendable once by 3 months \[[186](#o186)\].

> **Need to know**
> - Where does KHDA publish the list of free-zone universities allowed to sponsor student visas?

### Step 2: Apply through the university

1. **Get an unconditional offer and pay what the university asks before the visa.** Middlesex University Dubai, for example, wants 25% of the year's tuition paid first, a passport valid one year, and a parent's no-objection letter for anyone under 18 \[[N62](#n62)\].
2. **Send the university your papers.** You'll need \[[195](#o195), [82 (2024)](#o82)\]:

   - passport and photo;
   - the university's enrolment certificate and acceptance letter stating the programme and its length;
   - the Ministry of Education's or the emirate education authority's approval certificate;
   - valid health insurance.

3. **The university applies as your sponsor** through ICP's "Issuing Residency Permit" service (ICP Smart Services website or ICP Smart App) and charges you its visa fee (examples below) \[[42 (2022)](#o42), [82 (2024)](#o82)\].
4. **Check the visa before you fly:** ICP's File Validity page for Abu Dhabi and the Northern Emirates, GDRFA's Status Inquiry page for Dubai (see Tourist visa, Step 4) \[[95](#o95)\]. Track an ICP application at smartservices.icp.gov.ae with the application number starting 010 \[[83](#o83)\].
5. **Finish the residence within 60 days of entry,** or overstay fines apply \[[82 (2024)](#o82)\].

> **Need to know**
> - Does a student need a UAE medical fitness test before the student residence is issued?

Fees are set by each university. Examples: Middlesex University Dubai charges AED 5,555.22 if you apply from outside the UAE (AED 7,235.22 inside), covering processing, Emirates ID and medical insurance, plus a refundable AED 2,500 deposit, and wants 25% of the year's tuition paid first \[[N62](#n62)\]. The American University in Dubai charges AED 2,000 plus a refundable AED 3,000 deposit, and its compulsory health insurance comes on top (AED 3,200, non-refundable, for a student starting in the autumn semester) \[[N63](#n63)\]. If the visa is refused the deposit comes back but the government fee may not \[[N62](#n62)\]. Leaving without cancelling the visa forfeits the deposit \[[N63](#n63)\].

The visa lasts for the programme; ICP lets a student stay 180 days after finishing, but some universities keep it active only 3 months after graduation \[[82 (2024)](#o82), [N63](#n63)\].

### Can a student work?

Only through an employer's MOHRE student training and work permit: for students aged 15 and over already in the UAE, valid 3 months, AED 50 federal fee, with guardian consent if under 18, the institution's no-objection for training, and a medical certificate \[[197](#o197)\]. University students can also get MOHRE's free 2-year private-tutoring permit \[[32](#o32)\].

> **Need to know**
