> - Is there a weekly hours limit for university students working on the student training and work permit?

## Green visa

A green visa is a 5-year renewable residence with no employer sponsor, and the holder can sponsor family, including first-degree relatives \[[198](#o198), [42 (2022)](#o42)\]. Three routes:

- **Skilled worker:** MOHRE work permit under a valid contract, MOHRE skill level 1, 2 or 3, at least a bachelor's degree, and salary of at least AED 15,000 a month \[[199](#o199), [42 (2022)](#o42)\]. MOHRE does not count anyone earning under AED 4,000 or without a degree as skilled \[[185](#o185)\].
- **Freelancer or self-employed:** MOHRE freelance permit, a bachelor's degree or specialised diploma, and self-employment income of at least AED 360,000 a year for the past two years or proof of financial solvency throughout the stay \[[42 (2022)](#o42), [32](#o32)\]; In Dubai, GDRFA requires both the income and proof of solvency \[[200](#o200)\]. Abu Dhabi's Residents Office is not taking nominations for this category \[[201](#o201)\].
- **Investor or partner:** a trade licence and partnership or investment contract with a paid share of at least AED 1 million \[[202](#o202), [82 (2024)](#o82)\].

**How to apply in Dubai (GDRFA):**

1. **Get the MOHRE permit first:** the work permit (skilled worker) or the self-employed permit (freelancer) \[[199](#o199), [200](#o200)\].
2. **Apply for the green visa entry permit.** You'll need \[[199](#o199), [200](#o200), [202](#o202)\]:

   - a personal photo and a passport copy valid at least 6 months;
   - skilled worker: the MOHRE permit, and the employment contract if the employer is government, semi-government or in a free zone;
   - freelancer: the MOHRE self-employed permit;
   - investor or partner: the partnership or investment contract and the trade licence.

   1. Log in to GDRFA smart services (smart.gdrfad.gov.ae) with UAE Pass or a username \[[199](#o199)\].
   2. Search for the green visa service for your category, fill in the application and pay \[[199](#o199), [200](#o200)\].

   Or go to a GDRFA Customer Happiness Centre or an Amer centre, take a ticket and hand in the application and papers \[[199](#o199)\].

3. **Pay** AED 200 plus 5% VAT, plus AED 520 if you are inside the UAE \[[199](#o199)\]. GDRFA expects the skilled-worker entry visa in 48 hours \[[199](#o199)\].
4. **Finish the residence within 60 days of entry** \[[199](#o199)\]; it is linked to an Emirates ID application \[[82 (2024)](#o82)\]. ICP charges AED 100 to apply, AED 100 per year and AED 100 smart service fee \[[82 (2024)](#o82)\].

**Check it is real:** check the permit on GDRFA's Status Inquiry page (Dubai) or ICP's File Validity page (other emirates), as in Work visa: company worker, Step 5 \[[95](#o95)\].

> **Need to know**
> - Outside Dubai, what is ICP's service name and fee for the green visa entry permit, and how long does it take?

> **Need to know**
> - Are green visas being approved for Bangladeshi skilled workers in 2026?

## Golden visa

A golden visa is a 5- or 10-year renewable residence with no sponsor; the holder may stay abroad more than six months and sponsor a spouse, children of any age and parents \[[203](#o203), [42 (2022)](#o42)\]. Employers hire a golden visa holder on a 2-year golden visa work permit \[[32](#o32)\].

**How to apply:**

1. **Check you qualify** with ICP's "Check your eligibility for Golden visa" on smartservices.icp.gov.ae; Dubai also has GDRFA's golden visa services, and creatives ask the Ministry of Culture for a recommendation at mcy.gov.ae/en/goldenvisa \[[203](#o203)\].
2. **Apply directly on ICP's platform, or be nominated** by a federal or local authority \[[42 (2022)](#o42)\]. You'll need your passport, a personal photo and proof of eligibility for your category \[[82 (2024)](#o82)\].
3. **Pay ICP** AED 100 to apply, AED 100 per year and AED 100 smart service fee \[[82 (2024)](#o82)\].
4. **If you are abroad,** you get a 6-month multiple-entry visa, renewable once, to come and finish the steps \[[42 (2022)](#o42)\].

**Check it is real:** apply only through ICP or GDRFA, never a consultancy; check on icp.gov.ae or call 600522222 (see Scam below) \[[204](#o204)\].

**Categories** \[[203](#o203)\]:

- **Skilled professional (10 years):** MOHRE work permit, skill level 1 or 2, bachelor's degree, salary of at least AED 30,000 a month, and a practice licence where required \[[42 (2022)](#o42)\]. In Dubai without a government nomination, the AED 30,000 must be basic salary excluding allowances (since November 2025), and since December 2025 it must also have been earned for the previous two years \[[N64](#n64)\].
- **Investor:** AED 2 million in a fund, bank deposit or company, or a company paying AED 250,000 a year in federal tax (10 years); or property worth AED 2 million, wholly owned or with an approved local bank loan (5 years) \[[203](#o203), [42 (2022)](#o42)\].
- **Entrepreneur (5 years):** an approved innovative project \[[203](#o203)\].
- **Exceptional talent, scientists and specialists (10 years):** by recommendation \[[203](#o203)\].
- **Outstanding students and graduates:** one-time; for example a GPA of 3.5 from an A-ranked UAE university, 3.8 from a B-ranked one, or 3.5 from a top-100 world university, within two years of graduating \[[42 (2022)](#o42)\].
- **Humanitarian pioneers and frontline heroes (10 years):** including nurses, paramedics and lab and pharmacy technicians accredited to the National Pride Office \[[203](#o203), [42 (2022)](#o42)\].

**Status for Bangladeshis:** Bangladeshis have received golden residence, many through investment or business \[[N65](#n65)\]; journalist Keramat Ullah Biplob got a 10-year golden residence in the "expert journalist" category in March 2025, after actor Shakib Khan and cricketers Ashraful and Tamim Iqbal \[[N65](#n65)\].

**Scam:** on 7 July 2025 Bangladeshi media reported a cheap "nomination-based" golden visa for Bangladeshis and Indians \[[N66](#n66)\]. ICP denied it the next day: golden visas go only through official UAE channels, no consultancy inside or outside the UAE is authorised, and nobody should pay fees or hand over documents to anyone selling one \[[204](#o204)\]. Check eligibility on icp.gov.ae or call 600522222 \[[204](#o204)\].

## Other residence visas (freelance, partner or investor, remote work, retirement, property owner)

- **Freelance permit:** MOHRE issues it to people working independently, including foreigners on self-sponsored residence, without an employer or contract \[[32](#o32)\]. It is the first requirement for the self-employed green visa \[[200](#o200)\].
- **Partner or investor visa through a trade licence:** a free zone company issues residence visas to its shareholders. In DMCC (Dubai Multi Commodities Centre, a Dubai free zone), a share certificate of at least 50 shares totalling AED 50,000 gives a 3-year investor visa with no immigration deposit \[[186](#o186)\]. The visa is tied to that free zone and does not allow work on the mainland \[[186](#o186)\], and the number of visas a company gets depends on its office size (a DMCC flexi desk: up to 3) \[[186](#o186)\]. ICP inspects for people brought in under one sponsor who work for others \[[42 (2022)](#o42)\].
- **Remote work visa:** one year, renewable, no sponsor; needs health insurance, medical fitness, proof of working for a company outside the UAE and a salary of at least USD 3,500 a month \[[205](#o205), [206](#o206)\].
- **Retirement visa:** 5 years, for people who worked at least 15 years and are 55 or older, with property worth AED 1 million plus AED 1 million savings, or income of AED 180,000 a year (AED 240,000 in Dubai) \[[207](#o207)\].
- **Property-owner visa:** a fully built, wholly owned, habitable property registered with the land authority, plus income of at least AED 10,000 a month or proof of solvency \[[82 (2024)](#o82)\].

**How to apply:**

1. **Freelance or investor/partner green visa:** see Green visa, How to apply \[[200](#o200), [202](#o202)\].
2. **Remote work visa:** apply on ICP's smart services or with GDRFA Dubai \[[205](#o205)\]. GDRFA wants a colour photo, passport copy, health insurance, medical fitness result, proof of remote work outside the UAE, a USD 3,500 salary certificate and the ID receipt; its fee is AED 200, plus AED 10 and AED 10, plus AED 500 if you are inside the UAE and AED 20 delivery \[[206](#o206)\]. The steps are the same as the green visa: log in to GDRFA smart services, search, fill in and pay, or go to a Customer Happiness Centre or Amer centre \[[199](#o199), [206](#o206)\].
3. **Retirement visa:** ICP's "Issue residence entry permit for foreigner retired" or GDRFA's "Issuing a residence permit for the retired foreigner" \[[207](#o207)\]. ICP wants your passport, photo, a utility bill, a work certificate showing your retirement and years of service, and either the municipality's property ownership certificate with a valuation certificate from the competent authority or a bank certificate of fixed annual income with 6 months' statements, plus health insurance \[[82 (2024)](#o82)\].
4. **Property-owner visa:** ICP's "Issuing Residency Permit" service, on the ICP Smart Services website or app \[[82 (2024)](#o82)\].

> **Need to know**
> - What do Bangladeshis pay, all in, for a "partner visa" through a free-zone or mainland trade licence in 2026?
> - Which free zones are refusing or holding partner visas for Bangladeshi nationals in 2026?
> - What happens to a partner-visa holder who does not renew the trade licence: is the visa cancelled and is there a fine?
> - Who qualifies for the 10-year Blue residence, and can Bangladeshis get it?

## Changing visa type

- **Visit visa to family or employment residence:** The law lets an entry visa be converted to residence without leaving the UAE after paying the fee \[[42 (2022)](#o42)\]; ICP charges AED 500 for the status change, and in Dubai GDRFA charges AED 500 for a person inside the country \[[82 (2024)](#o82), [182](#o182)\]. For Bangladeshis, turning a visit visa into a work visa has been closed for several years, and new employment visas and company-to-company transfers have been restricted since July 2024 \[[N28](#n28), [N43](#n43)\].
- **Family residence to work:** Yes, without changing visa. The employer gets a MOHRE dependant work permit (see Family residence) \[[185](#o185)\].
- **Employment to family residence:** Yes, if the spouse or parent qualifies to sponsor; the new residence is issued after the old one is cancelled, for AED 500 inside the country \[[82 (2024)](#o82)\].
- **Expired or cancelled residence to new residence:** Yes, without leaving, after paying the fee \[[42 (2022)](#o42)\].
- **Employment to green or golden visa:** Yes, if you meet the salary, skill-level and degree thresholds \[[199](#o199), [42 (2022)](#o42)\].
- **Student to work:** Only with a work permit; a foreigner may not work, paid or unpaid, without one \[[42 (2022)](#o42)\]. A student may stay 180 days after study ends \[[82 (2024)](#o82)\].

**How to change status inside the UAE:**

1. **Do not let your stay lapse.** After a residence is cancelled or expires most people have 30 days' grace \[[34](#o34)\].
2. **The new sponsor gets the new visa** (an employer through the Work Bundle; a family sponsor as in Family residence, Step 3) \[[82 (2024)](#o82)\].
3. **The sponsor files the change of status inside the country** with ICP, or GDRFA in Dubai, and pays AED 500; the old residence must already be cancelled, and any fines must be settled \[[82 (2024)](#o82), [182](#o182)\].
4. **Finish the new residence** (medical test, Emirates ID, residence) as that visa's section describes; the residence is linked to an Emirates ID application \[[82 (2024)](#o82)\].

**Check it is real:** check the new permit on ICP's File Validity page or GDRFA's Status Inquiry page (see Tourist visa, Step 4) \[[95](#o95)\].

> **Need to know**
> - Is GDRFA or ICP refusing in-country status changes for Bangladeshi passport holders in 2026?
> - Can a worker on a 3-month mission work visa be moved to a two-year employment residence without leaving the UAE?

## Asylum

The UAE is not party to the 1951 Refugee Convention or its 1967 Protocol, and its law gives no asylum or refugee status \[[N67](#n67), [N68 (2024)](#n68)\]. Anyone without legal residence is treated under immigration law and can be detained; some people seeking protection have been held in a section of the airport while waiting for resettlement elsewhere \[[N68 (2024)](#n68)\]. People without legal residence, including those on visit or expired visas, generally cannot work legally \[[N68 (2024)](#n68)\]. The refugees UNHCR (the UN refugee agency) recognises in the UAE come mainly from Iraq, Syria and Palestine; it counts 1,377 refugees and 6,257 asylum seekers \[[N67](#n67)\].

> **Need to know**
> - Does UNHCR's office in Abu Dhabi register new asylum claims, and how is it contacted?

## Having a child in the UAE

The baby's residence is a family residence, so the parent must meet the family-sponsorship salary and housing rules \[[142](#o142)\].

**Unmarried parents.** A man and an adult woman who have a child outside marriage face at least 2 years' jail, but no case is brought if they marry, or one or both acknowledge the child and get the child identity and travel documents from their country \[[208 (2021)](#o208)\]. The birth certificate needs a marriage certificate or the parents' acknowledgement of the child legalised by their embassy or a notary \[[209 (2022)](#o209)\]; if the father is unknown, a court order based on the mother's acknowledgement is needed \[[209 (2022)](#o209)\].

1. **Get the birth certificate within 30 days** at the hospital or the emirate's health authority (the Ministry of Health and Prevention, MOHAP; the Dubai Health Authority, DHA, in Dubai; the Department of Health, DoH, in Abu Dhabi) \[[210](#o210)\]. You'll need the attested marriage certificate translated into Arabic, both parents' passports (originals and copies), the hospital's birth notification and discharge summary \[[210](#o210)\]. Late reporting can be fined up to AED 5,000 \[[209 (2022)](#o209)\].
2. **Attest it** with MOHAP (a DHA or DoH stamp counts) and the UAE MOFA \[[210](#o210), [211](#o211)\]; the UAE MOFA fee is AED 150 \[[211](#o211)\].
3. **Register the birth with Bangladesh.**
   - Dubai Consulate: apply online at bdris.gov.bd, then take the printed form, 2 photos, parents' passport copies, the child's UAE birth certificate attested by UAE MOFA, and the marriage certificate attested by MoFA Dhaka to Foshwa Global, 3 Reem Residency, Umm Hurair, Karama, Dubai (appointment needed; 8 am-4 pm Mon-Fri; +971 4 288 0190, WhatsApp +971 50 729 2294) \[[212](#o212), [N69](#n69)\]. Free within 45 days, then AED 10, plus AED 31.50 service charge; certificate in 3 working days \[[N69](#n69), [212](#o212)\].
   - Abu Dhabi: parents' digital birth certificates, the child's photo and original UAE birth certificate and parents' original passports to Room 102 at the Embassy; free within 45 days, then AED 5, cash only, 15 days \[[213](#o213)\].
4. **Get the child a Bangladeshi passport.** Apply online at epassport.gov.bd, then go to the Consulate or Embassy with the 17-digit online-verifiable birth certificate in English, two 3R lab photos on white, the UAE birth certificate in English and the parents' passports or NIDs; children under 5 need not attend \[[214](#o214)\]. A first passport needs police verification in Bangladesh; otherwise a regular passport takes 4-6 weeks \[[214](#o214)\]. Missions charge general applicants USD 100 (express USD 150) for a 48-page, 5-year passport \[[86](#o86)\]; in Abu Dhabi that was AED 405 (express AED 610), cash only \[[215 (2024)](#o215)\].
5. **Get the residence visa within 120 days.** The baby's passport, Emirates ID and residence must be done within 120 days of birth; after that AED 100 a day is charged and the baby cannot leave the UAE \[[210](#o210)\].

In Dubai a newborn is covered under the mother's health insurance for its first 30 days \[[216 (2020)](#o216)\].

> **Need to know**
> - What does a normal delivery cost at a government hospital for an uninsured or basic-insured Bangladeshi mother in Dubai and Abu Dhabi?
> - What does the Dubai Consulate charge in dirhams for a child's e-passport (the official mission rate is USD 100, express USD 150 \[[86](#o86)\])?
> - Is the Consulate service centre's (Foshwa Global, Umm Hurair) phone +971 4 288 0190 \[[N69](#n69)\] or +971 4 570 1648 \[[217](#o217)\], or does each serve a different desk?

## Sending money home

**Use a bank or a licensed exchange house, never hundi** (sending through an unlicensed agent) \[[57](#o57)\]. Bangladesh's Financial Intelligence Unit (BFIU) had suspended or frozen about 55,000 mobile-money (MFS) accounts linked to digital hundi and online gambling by June 2026, and decided in August 2026 to freeze about 14,000 more \[[N70](#n70)\].

The UAE was Bangladesh's third-largest source of remittances in July-September 2026, USD 777 million, after Saudi Arabia and the UK \[[27](#o27)\]. It sent USD 4,636 million in FY2023-24, USD 4,168 million in FY2024-25 and USD 4,584 million in FY2025-26, out of USD 35,589 million from all countries in FY2025-26 \[[26](#o26)\]. Sending from the UAE is the most expensive of Bangladesh's ten main corridors: 2.92% of the amount on average in April-June 2026, and 6.49% for transfers up to USD 200 (about AED 735) \[[218](#o218), [2](#o2)\].

You'll need:
- the receiver's bKash number (bKash is a Bangladeshi mobile-money account) and their full name exactly as given when the account was opened \[[N71](#n71)\].

1. **Check the licence.** Exchange houses must be licensed by the Central Bank of the UAE (CBUAE). Open centralbank.ae/en/licensing, "Search Financial Institutions", choose licence type "Exchange Business" and look for the name on the shop's sign \[[219](#o219)\]. Licensed examples include Lulu International Exchange, Al Fardan Exchange, Al Rostamani International Exchange, Sharaf Exchange, Index Exchange, GCC Exchange, Al Ahalia Money Exchange Bureau, Al Dahab Exchange and Unimoni Exchange \[[219](#o219)\]. A shop or person not on the register is not licensed \[[219](#o219)\].
2. **Send.** For bKash, bKash lists these UAE partners: Al Ahalia Exchange, Al Ansari, Al Dahab Exchange, Al Fardan Exchange, Al Rostamani, GCC Remit, Index Exchange, Ria, e&money, Wise, Worldwide CE, Western Union, Taptap Send, MoneyGram and Remitly \[[N71](#n71)\]. At the counter give the bKash number and registered name and hand over the cash; up to Tk 2.5 lakh per transfer, any number of times \[[N71](#n71)\]. 
3. **The family** gets the government's 2.5% cash incentive on money sent through legal channels, kept in the FY2026-27 budget \[[18](#o18)\], and can cash out bKash remittance at Tk 7 per thousand at BRAC Bank, City Bank and Q-Cash ATMs \[[N71](#n71)\].

> **Need to know**
> - What ID does a UAE exchange house ask for to send money (Emirates ID only, or is a passport enough)?
> - What reference number does a UAE exchange-house receipt carry, and how does the family trace a transfer that hasn't arrived?
> - Where does a worker in the UAE complain when a licensed exchange house loses or delays a transfer (CBUAE's consumer complaint channel)?
> - Can a worker whose residence visa has expired or been cancelled still send money legally from a UAE exchange house without a valid Emirates ID?

**Saving at home: the Wage Earner Development Bond.** A 5-year government bond for workers abroad, bought in taka or foreign currency in the worker's own name or a named beneficiary in Bangladesh, at authorised-dealer bank branches in Bangladesh or at Bangladeshi banks' branches and exchange houses abroad \[[220](#o220)\]. Denominations run from Tk 25,000 to Tk 50 lakh, with no upper limit on purchases \[[220](#o220)\]. It pays 12% simple profit at maturity on holdings up to Tk 15 lakh (11% for Tk 15-30 lakh, 10% for Tk 30-50 lakh, 9% above), paid every six months; the money and profit are income-tax free, you can borrow against it, name a nominee, and take the principal back in US dollars \[[220](#o220)\].

> **Need to know**
> - Which UAE exchange houses sell the Wage Earner Development Bond, and what papers does a worker need there?

## Going home without valid papers

If your visa or residence permit has expired or been cancelled, the UAE fines you AED 50 (about Tk 1,700) for every day you stay after the grace period \[[34](#o34), [123](#o123)\]. The grace period after a residence permit expires or is cancelled is 30 days for most workers, 60 days for permits held with a guarantor or host, 90 days for skilled workers at levels 1-3, and 180 days for Golden, Green and Blue residence holders \[[34](#o34)\]. To leave you pay the fines, get an exit (departure) permit, and fly out within 7 days \[[124](#o124)\]. Not paying the fine can bring up to 3 months' prison or a fine of up to AED 4,000 (about Tk 1.36 lakh), and the court may deport you \[[221 (2021)](#o221)\].

The last general amnesty (a period when overstay fines are waived) ran from 1 September to 31 December 2024 \[[222 (2024)](#o222), [223 (2024)](#o223), [224 (2024)](#o224)\]; about 50,000 Bangladeshis regularised and more than 5,500 went home on travel permits without a ban \[[N72](#n72)\]. After it ended the UAE announced inspections and arrests of violators from January 2025 \[[N72](#n72)\]. In 2026 ICP (the Federal Authority for Identity, Citizenship, Customs and Port Security, the federal immigration authority) waived only fines run up from 28 February 2026 by people stranded by the regional airspace closures, then gave them a final 30-day grace period, 10 June to 9 July 2026, to leave or regularise \[[225](#o225), [226](#o226), [227](#o227)\].

> **Need to know**
> - Is a new UAE amnesty expected for 2026-2027, as Bangladeshi missions have asked?

### Step 1: Get a passport or outpass

With a valid passport, go to Step 2 \[[131](#o131)\]. If your passport has expired, been lost, damaged or mutilated, or you have lived in the UAE illegally for a long time, get a Travel Permit (an "outpass", a one-way document home) from the Bangladesh mission \[[131](#o131)\]. It is valid 3 months \[[131](#o131)\]. The Dubai Consulate issued 2,915 travel permits from January to June 2026 \[[228](#o228)\].

Only holders of a Bangladeshi machine-readable passport (MRP) or e-passport can get one; the Consulate can't issue it on an old hand-written passport \[[131](#o131)\].

You'll need \[[131](#o131)\]:
- the Travel Permit (Out Pass) application form (download it from bcgdubai.gov.bd, "Download Forms") \[[229 (2024)](#o229)\];
- a photocopy of your expired MRP;
- if the passport is lost, the police lost report (see "If you lose your passport") and a photocopy of the lost passport;
- two passport-size colour photos;
- your email address and phone number written on the form;
- your Emirates ID, if you have one.

1. Go yourself to the Consulate General of Bangladesh in Dubai, Villa No. 36 & 145, Abdulla Hussain Al Malik Villa, 123/3 Street, Abu Hail Road, Al Wuheida, Deira \[[131](#o131)\]. Hand in the papers at counter No. 19, Monday to Thursday 9 am to 3 pm, Friday from 9 am (the Consulate closes at 11:30 am on Fridays) \[[131](#o131), [230](#o230)\].
2. Pay AED 60 (about Tk 2,040) in cash at counter 19; it is not refunded if refused \[[131](#o131)\].
3. Collect it the same day: Monday to Thursday 3 to 4 pm, Friday 12 to 12:30 pm \[[131](#o131)\].

The Embassy in Abu Dhabi also issues travel permits (address and hours in "Contacts") \[[60](#o60)\].

> **Need to know**
> - What does the Abu Dhabi Embassy charge for a travel permit, and is it also same-day?
> - How does a worker who never had an MRP or e-passport (only a hand-written passport, or no passport at all) prove citizenship to get a travel permit?

**If it fails:** Dubai Consulate labour welfare helplines +971 50 816 8253 and +971 50 816 8363, consular and passport +971 50 505 5834, all WhatsApp text only \[[231 (2024)](#o231)\]; Abu Dhabi Embassy labour wing +971 2 445 8182 or labourwingauh@gmail.com \[[132](#o132)\].

### Step 2: Settle fines and status

1. **Check your fines.** In Dubai use the Fines Inquiry Service of GDRFA (Dubai's immigration office), gdrfad.gov.ae/en/fines-inquiry-service, with your passport number \[[232](#o232)\]. For any emirate, call ICP on 600522222 (+971600522222 from abroad) \[[233](#o233)\].
2. **Check for a travel ban.** An unpaid debt, such as a bounced cheque, can bring a travel ban that stops you leaving even after the fines are paid \[[N40](#n40)\]. Dubai Police's free "Circulars and travel bans" service on the Dubai Police app or website (log in with UAE Pass, the national digital ID), at smart police stations or at police stations shows at once whether a financial-case circular or travel ban exists \[[234](#o234)\]. In Abu Dhabi, the Abu Dhabi Judicial Department (ADJD, the emirate's courts and prosecution) has an Estafser inquiry page, adjd.gov.ae/sites/eServices/EN/Pages/Estafser.aspx, that asks only for your UID number and a captcha code, with no login \[[235](#o235)\]; ADJD answers on 600 599 799, +971 2 651 2222 from abroad, WhatsApp +971 2 600 599 799 or info@adjd.gov.ae \[[235](#o235)\].
3. **Check for a labour ban or absconding report.** Call MOHRE (the Ministry of Human Resources and Emiratisation) on 600590000, 8 am to 8 pm \[[33](#o33)\]. A proven "work abandonment" report (the official name for an absconding report) bars a new work permit for a year, counted from the day you leave; workers on a family visa, workers rejoining the same company, skilled workers and Golden visa holders are exempt \[[33](#o33)\]. An employer who reports that a worker has left work is exempt from the AED 50,000 fine for employing or harbouring violators \[[221 (2021)](#o221)\].
4. **Pay the fines.** Online on ICP's smart services site or app (UAE Pass login, plus an AED 100 smart-services fee), or at an ICP Customer Happiness Centre \[[123](#o123)\]. After paying you must either regularise (get a new visa) or leave \[[123](#o123)\].

> **Need to know**
> - Does ADJD's Estafser show Abu Dhabi travel bans or only court cases, and is the UID it asks for the unified number printed on the UAE visa?
> - Can a worker with no UAE Pass (because their Emirates ID has been cancelled) check and pay fines online, or only at a centre?
> - Who pays the fines when the employer let the visa lapse: can a worker claim them from the employer at MOHRE?

### Step 3: Leave

You'll need \[[124](#o124), [236](#o236)\]:
- your passport, or the travel permit from Step 1;
- a personal photo;
- a confirmed air ticket dated within 7 days;
- the fine payment.

1. **Get the exit permit.** Outside Dubai, apply on ICP's smart services (UAE Pass) or at an ICP Customer Happiness Centre; AED 200 plus AED 100 smart-services fee (about Tk 10,200); ready in 2 days \[[124](#o124)\]. In Dubai, go yourself to an Amer centre, GDRFA's Public Services Section in Al Aweer, the Terminal 3 airport service centre or Hatta port; violators also have an eye-print recorded; AED 241 (about Tk 8,200); about 48 hours \[[236](#o236)\].
2. **Fly within 7 days** of the permit's issue. If you miss it, the permit must be cancelled and a new one applied for, and from the 8th day after it expires you are fined AED 50 a day again \[[124](#o124)\].

> **Need to know**
> - Can a violator pay fines and get the exit permit at the airport on the day of travel, outside Dubai's Terminal 3 centre?

### If you lose your passport

You'll need \[[214](#o214), [237](#o237)\]:
- a copy of the lost passport;
- your NID (national ID card) or 17-digit online birth registration certificate \[[214](#o214)\];
- your Emirates ID and UAE visa copy, if you have them \[[214](#o214)\].

1. **Report the loss to the police.** In Dubai, use "Report Lost Item" on the Dubai Police app or website (UAE Pass) or at a police station, 7:30 am to 10 pm \[[237](#o237)\]. The Certificate of Loss costs AED 50 plus AED 20 in fees (about Tk 2,400), AED 100 more at a police station, takes up to 3 working days, is emailed to you and is valid one month \[[237](#o237)\].
2. **If you want to stay and work:** apply for a new e-passport. Fill the form at epassport.gov.bd, book the appointment, then go yourself to the Consulate with the printed form, the police report, a copy of the old passport, your NID or birth certificate and your Emirates ID and visa; pay at counters 1-3 and give fingerprints and iris scan in Room 23 \[[214](#o214)\]. It takes 4-6 weeks (urgent about 15 days) if no police verification is needed and your data match your NID \[[214](#o214)\]. Missions charge labourers and students less than other applicants: 48 pages, 5 years USD 30 (express USD 45); 10 years USD 50 (USD 75) \[[85](#o85), [86](#o86)\]. Other applicants pay USD 100 (express USD 150) for 48 pages, 5 years; the Abu Dhabi Embassy charged that as AED 405 \[[86](#o86), [215 (2024)](#o215)\].
3. **If you are going home:** get a travel permit instead (Step 1); ICP issues the exit permit on the consulate's travel pass and the police report \[[124](#o124)\].

> **Need to know**
> - Do the Dubai Consulate and Abu Dhabi Embassy charge workers the USD 30 (express USD 45) labourer rate, and how much is that in dirhams at the counter?
> - How does a worker outside Dubai get the police lost report (Abu Dhabi, Sharjah and the northern emirates)?

### If you are detained

The UAE can hold a person under a deportation order for up to 30 days, extendable once \[[221 (2021)](#o221)\]. The deportee's own money pays for the deportation, or the employer if it broke the law, otherwise the state \[[221 (2021)](#o221)\]. GDRFA's centre in Al Aweer (Dubai) handles residency violators, including issuing exit permits with an eye-print \[[236](#o236)\].

**What the detainee does.**

1. **Ask to contact the Bangladesh mission.** Its labour wings visit jails and deportation centres to identify Bangladeshis \[[60](#o60), [59 (2021)](#o59)\]. Dubai Consulate labour welfare (WhatsApp text): +971 50 816 8253, +971 50 816 8363 \[[231 (2024)](#o231)\]; Abu Dhabi Embassy labour wing: +971 2 445 8182, labourwingauh@gmail.com \[[132](#o132)\].
2. **Get a travel permit if you have no valid passport.** The Embassy's form asks for your passport number, sponsor, arrival date, the cause of jail and the sentence, with two photos \[[130](#o130)\]; papers and fee are in Step 1 \[[131](#o131)\].
3. **Ask the labour wing for the ticket if you have no money.** The Abu Dhabi labour wing gives air tickets or money to workers in distress \[[60](#o60)\].
4. **Say if you are a Welfare Board (WEWB) member** (see "If a worker dies, is injured or loses the job"): members get the Dubai Consulate's help to be freed and brought home \[[217](#o217)\].

**What the family does.**

1. **Call the Probashi call centre,** 16135 (+8809610102030 from abroad), 24 hours \[[68](#o68)\].
2. **Apply to the Welfare Board for legal help abroad** ("If your family in Bangladesh asks for help", step 2) \[[238](#o238)\]. To ask the Ministry to help bring the worker home, have the worker's passport copy, visa, UAE workplace and home address, the agency's name and address and the employer's mobile number \[[239](#o239)\]. Check membership on portal.wewb.gov.bd/bmet-wewbm-check with the passport number \[[101](#o101)\].
3. **Write to the labour wing** with the same details: Abu Dhabi labourwingauh@gmail.com \[[132](#o132)\]; Dubai labour.dubai@mofa.gov.bd \[[240](#o240)\].
4. **No reply:** the Ministry's grievance officer, adsadmin@probashi.gov.bd, 02-41030224 \[[239](#o239)\].

> **Need to know**
> - Where is the deportation centre for Abu Dhabi and the northern emirates, and can family visit?
> - Who buys the ticket home for a detained worker with no money: the employer, the mission or the deportation centre?
> - How long does a Bangladeshi held for overstay usually wait in Al Aweer before the flight?
> - Will UAE police or the Al Aweer centre tell the Bangladesh mission about a detained Bangladeshi if the detainee asks, and what number should the detainee give them?

### If your family in Bangladesh asks for help

A close relative can ask the Ministry of Expatriates' Welfare and Overseas Employment to help bring a worker home; it acts through the mission, the Welfare Board or BMET, free, within 7 working days \[[239](#o239)\].

You'll need \[[239](#o239)\]:
- proof the person is a migrant worker (passport copy, visa, Emirates ID);
- their workplace and home address in the UAE;
- the recruiting agency's name and address;
- the employer's mobile number.

1. Call the Probashi call centre (run by the Wage Earners' Welfare Board, WEWB), 16135 free, or +8809610102030 from abroad, 24 hours \[[68](#o68)\].
2. For legal help abroad (a case, detention, unpaid dues), apply to the Welfare Board on myGov (mygov.bd), at the Board (Probashi Kallyan Bhaban, 71-72 Eskaton Garden, Ramna, Dhaka), or through the expatriate welfare branch of the Deputy Commissioner's office or the DEMO (District Employment and Manpower Office); the Board writes to the labour wing in 1-2 days, which takes about 15 working days; free \[[241](#o241), [238](#o238), [242](#o242)\]. This needs BMET clearance or Board membership \[[241](#o241)\].
3. No reply: the Ministry's grievance officer, Joint Secretary (Admin and Finance), 02-41030224, 01712992086, adsadmin@probashi.gov.bd, has 30 working days \[[239](#o239)\].

### Bans after you leave

A person who has been deported can't return to the UAE without the permission of ICP's chairman \[[221 (2021)](#o221)\]. Under past amnesties, people who left on an amnesty exit permit had no "entry ban stamp" \[[224 (2024)](#o224)\]; in the 2018 amnesty, people who had entered the UAE illegally left with a two-year entry ban \[[243 (2018)](#o243)\]. A proven work abandonment report bars a new work permit for one year from the day you leave; skilled workers and Golden visa holders are exempt \[[33](#o33)\]. Forging or using a forged visa or residence permit can bring up to 10 years' prison and deportation \[[221 (2021)](#o221)\]. A Foreign Ministry official says a cancelled residence or a broken law bars re-entry for 5 to 10 years \[[N26 (2024)](#n26)\]; the protesters deported in 2024 had their fingerprints kept and may not get into the UAE or other Gulf states again \[[N26 (2024)](#n26)\].

To check or contest a labour ban, see "If you want to change employer" \[[33](#o33)\].

> **Need to know**
> - Does a worker who pays overstay fines and leaves on an ordinary exit permit (outside an amnesty) get an entry ban, and for how long?
> - How can a worker in Bangladesh check whether they have a UAE entry ban before paying for a new visa?
> - How does a deportee apply for the ICP chairman's permission to return, and has any Bangladeshi worker succeeded?

## If a worker dies, is injured or loses the job

In Bangladesh the Wage Earners' Welfare Board (the Welfare Board; Probashi Kallyan Bhaban, 71-72 Eskaton Garden, Ramna, Dhaka) pays grants and handles the insurance bought at BMET clearance \[[242](#o242), [244](#o244)\]. Most grants need either BMET emigration clearance (the smart card) or Welfare Board membership \[[245](#o245), [246](#o246)\]. Bangladeshis who went to the UAE on their own, often on visit visas, have no BMET clearance; Bangladesh missions register them for Welfare Board membership so they come under the government database and its welfare services \[[N28](#n28), [217](#o217)\].

**Get Welfare Board membership if you have no BMET card.** Any Bangladeshi aged 18 to 65 in the UAE without a BMET card can register for membership (the "Probashi Kallyan Card") \[[217](#o217)\]. The Dubai Consulate issued 5,028 cards from January to June 2026 \[[228](#o228)\].

You'll need \[[217](#o217)\]:
- your Bangladeshi passport;
- your UAE visa;
- your Emirates ID;
- one colour passport-size photo;
- your nominee's details.

1. Apply online at portal.wewb.gov.bd and print the form \[[247](#o247), [217](#o217)\].
2. Hand it in with the papers and AED 200 (the UAE rate set for the Tk 6,500 membership fee, including life insurance) in cash at the Consulate's service centre, Foshwa Global, No. 3 Reem Residency, Umm Hurair, Karama, Dubai (+971 4 570 1648, WhatsApp +971 50 729 2294); the fee is non-refundable \[[217](#o217), [247](#o247)\].
3. Collect the card from the centre after at least 3 working days \[[217](#o217)\]. If you lose it, download it again from wewb.gov.bd with your passport number \[[217](#o217)\]. In Abu Dhabi, pay the AED 200 into the Embassy's or labour wing's bank account and hand in the application, papers and receipt; the labour wing grants membership after checking \[[248](#o248)\]. Workers who already have a BMET clearance card do not need membership \[[247](#o247)\].

> **Need to know**
> - How long does Welfare Board membership bought in the UAE last, and how is it renewed?
> - Does the Tk 3 lakh death grant cover a member who went to the UAE on a visit visa, given the Consulate's page says it is for workers who went legally \[[217](#o217)\] while the Board says clearance or membership \[[245](#o245)\]?

### If you are injured or fall ill at work

**In the UAE.**

1. **Get treatment** at any government or private facility; the employer pays all of it, including hospital, surgery, tests, medicine, rehabilitation, artificial limbs and transport, until you recover or a disability is confirmed \[[71 (2022)](#o71), [249](#o249)\]. Accidents on the usual route to or from work count \[[249](#o249)\].
2. **Make sure it is reported.** The employer must report to MOHRE within 48 hours, via its website, app or service centre \[[249](#o249), [114](#o114)\]. If it does not, call MOHRE on 600590000 or its toll-free Labour Claims and Advisory Centre, 80084 \[[250](#o250), [137](#o137)\].
3. **Pay while off.** Full wage up to 6 months, then half wage up to 6 more \[[249](#o249)\].
4. **Compensation**, due within 10 days of the medical report \[[249](#o249)\]:
   - total permanent disability: 24 months' basic wage, AED 18,000 to 200,000 \[[249](#o249)\];
   - partial: the disability percentage in Cabinet Resolution 33 of 2022 times that amount, same limits \[[249](#o249)\].

   Lost if the injury was self-inflicted, under alcohol or drugs, from a deliberate safety breach, or if you refused treatment \[[249](#o249)\].
5. **If the employer will not pay,** file a MOHRE labour complaint (If you aren't paid) \[[134](#o134)\]. The Abu Dhabi labour wing visits workers in hospital and records what each needs to fly home (an escort, a stretcher or wheelchair, a ticket from the sponsor or the Embassy) \[[60](#o60), [251](#o251)\].

**In Bangladesh.**

1. **Going home injured.** On your or your family's application, with proof of clearance or membership and a passport copy, the Welfare Board asks the mission to bring you home \[[252](#o252)\]. The Board's ambulance at Dhaka and Chattogram airports is free on request (Dhaka desk +8802 8901040, 01404 413388) \[[253](#o253)\].
2. **Treatment money in Bangladesh.** A returnee with clearance or membership who is injured, sick or disabled can get up to Tk 2 lakh; the family applies within 1 year of return through the Board's web portal, the district probashi kallyan (migrant welfare) centre or the Board \[[246](#o246), [242](#o242)\].

   You'll need \[[246](#o246), [242](#o242)\]:
   - medical reports and prescriptions;
   - a bank statement;
   - a passport copy and a copy of its arrival stamp;
   - proof of clearance or membership;
   - one 6R (6 by 8 inch) colour photo of the worker.

   A Board committee checks the case by WhatsApp video call; free; up to 60 working days after all papers are in \[[246](#o246), [242](#o242)\].
3. **Insurance.** For workers who left with clearance from 1 January 2023, it pays Tk 10 lakh for total permanent disability and 10% to 90% of that for partial permanent disability \[[254](#o254), [57](#o57)\]; claim through the Board (see "Claiming the insurance").

> **Need to know**
> - Is the treatment grant Tk 2 lakh (Welfare Board \[[246](#o246)\]) or Tk 1 lakh (Dubai Consulate's page \[[217](#o217)\])?

### If a worker dies

**In the UAE.**

1. **Police and death certificate.** A death outside hospital goes to the police, who arrange a post-mortem; the body is released with the prosecutor's permission \[[N73](#n73)\]. The death certificate is issued on the police registration, the hospital's death notification, passport and Emirates ID \[[N73](#n73)\].
2. **Consulate NOC.** The Embassy (Abu Dhabi) or Consulate (Dubai) issues the no-objection certificate; each mission's list of papers is under "The family in Bangladesh", step 3 \[[255](#o255), [256](#o256)\].

   Abu Dhabi queries: 02 445 8182 or WhatsApp 050 361 4760 \[[255](#o255)\].
3. **Embalming and flight.** In Dubai, Dubai Health's service needs the death certificate, notification, police letter, embassy letter, airline ticket and passport copy \[[257](#o257)\]; fees: embalming AED 1,020, ambulance AED 220, body-handling company AED 1,840, typing AED 52.50 \[[257](#o257)\]. The employer pays all costs of preparing and sending the body home if the family asks \[[10 (2021)](#o10)\].
4. **Money owed.** Within 10 days the employer must give the family all wages, dues and gratuity \[[10 (2021)](#o10)\]; a worker can name a family member in writing to receive them \[[10 (2021)](#o10)\]. For a work death the family also gets 24 months' basic wage, AED 18,000 to 200,000 \[[249](#o249)\]. The settlement can be paid to the Embassy or straight to the heirs \[[255](#o255)\]; the labour wing recovers death compensation from companies and courts \[[60](#o60)\]. Unpaid amounts up to AED 20,000 can come from the worker protection insurance \[[121](#o121)\].

> **Need to know**
> - How long does the Embassy take to pay a recovered death settlement to the family, and through which channel?

**The family in Bangladesh.**

These steps are for the family in Bangladesh. The Dubai Consulate handled 213 body repatriations from January to June 2026 \[[228](#o228)\].

1. **Ask the Welfare Board to bring the body home.** Call 16135 (from abroad +8809610102030) \[[68](#o68)\]. Then apply in one of these ways, even if the worker had no clearance or membership:
   - on myGov, service "Application to bring home a migrant worker's body", with a myGov account (register with your mobile number at idp-v2.live.mygov.bd/registration), the worker's passport and any clearance or membership; a tracking number comes by SMS; free; up to 35 working days \[[258](#o258)\]; or
   - to the Ministry of Expatriates' Welfare, aswelfare@probashi.gov.bd, which also handles irregular and undocumented workers, describing what happened and the cause of death, with proof the person was a migrant worker (passport, work permit, BMET smart card, Emirates ID) and the death certificate; free; 7 working days \[[239](#o239)\].
2. **Home or burial in the UAE.** The Board passes the family's choice to the mission \[[259](#o259), [260](#o260)\]. For burial in the UAE, apply on myGov, "Application for permission to bury a migrant worker's body in the destination country", with the worker's passport or visa copy, any clearance or membership, the district office's investigation report and a certificate signed by all heirs on the union parishad (rural council) chairman's letterhead; free; 3 working days \[[261](#o261)\]. The Abu Dhabi Embassy needs the family's no-objection certificate sent through the Welfare Board or the foreign ministry \[[255](#o255)\].
3. **The UAE side.** The Consulate issues a free No Objection Certificate (NOC) for the body the same day, Room 14, 9 am to 3 pm Monday to Thursday, 9 to 11:30 am Friday; hotline +971 50 816 8253 (WhatsApp only) \[[256](#o256)\]. The employer, sponsor, a colleague or a relative in the UAE applies with \[[256](#o256)\]:
   - the death certificate and the deceased's passport (originals and copies) and Emirates ID copy;
   - the death notification or medical report;
   - for an accidental death, the police or autopsy report;
   - the passport or Emirates ID copy and phone number of the person accompanying the body;
   - the family's contacts in Bangladesh, and the name and number of the person receiving the body at the airport;
   - proof that all money owed to the worker has been paid to the nominee.

   In Abu Dhabi the Embassy also asks for the cargo booking, the final settlement from the employer by cash or cheque in the Embassy's name (or sent to the heirs with the remittance copy), the NID copy of the airport receiver, and a Welfare Board letter or the heirs' application recommended by the local chairman \[[255](#o255)\]. The Board forwards the family's request within 3-5 working days and the labour wing takes about 30 \[[258](#o258)\].

4. **At the airport.** The Board's desk gives a Tk 35,000 cheque for transport and burial, collectable within 3 months, and a free ambulance \[[262](#o262), [253](#o253)\]. You'll need \[[263](#o263)\]:
   - the worker's passport copy;
   - a heirship (warish) certificate from the union parishad chairman, mayor or city councillor;
   - the NID of the wife, father or mother;
   - the death certificate;
   - the NOC;
   - the original air waybill;
   - proof of clearance or membership, if any.
5. **The Tk 3 lakh Welfare Board grant,** for the family of a worker with clearance or membership who died in the UAE, the country of the clearance \[[245](#o245)\]. If the family took the airport cheque, no application is needed; after burial in the UAE, apply within 1 year of the death to the Board's Director General or through the district office \[[245](#o245)\]. It goes to the spouse, children and parents; for an unmarried worker with no living parents, to full siblings \[[245](#o245)\].

   You'll need \[[245](#o245)\]:
   - a family-members certificate on the letterhead of the union parishad chairman, mayor or city councillor, countersigned by the UNO (Upazila Nirbahi Officer) or, in a city corporation, the regional executive officer;
   - an indemnity, undertaking and authorisation on Tk 400 non-judicial stamp paper, countersigned the same way (samples on wewb.gov.bd);
   - the NOC or death certificate copy;
   - a bank certificate or original statement for the receiver's account with the routing number;
   - each family member's colour photo, attested by the chairman and the district welfare centre;
   - the worker's passport copy.

   Paid by bank transfer (BEFTN, the Bangladesh Electronic Funds Transfer Network) with an SMS, within 60 working days of all papers reaching the Board \[[245](#o245), [242](#o242)\].
6. **The insurance:** Tk 10 lakh for a death within 5 years of the smart card date, for workers who left with clearance on or after 1 January 2023 \[[254](#o254), [242](#o242)\].

   > **Need to know**
   > - Are workers who left between 1 January and 11 March 2023 covered, since BMET's leaflet gives the start as 12 March 2023 \[[57](#o57)\]?
   > - Does Welfare Board membership bought in the UAE include the Tk 10 lakh death insurance, or a different sum?

7. **UAE compensation and dues.** When the mission reports that compensation, unpaid wages or end-of-service benefits may be due, the Welfare Board asks the family for a power of attorney and papers (with free help from its legal cell), sends them to the mission and follows the case; it pays the heirs within 60 working days of having the papers and the money \[[264](#o264), [242](#o242)\]. The Abu Dhabi labour wing recovers death compensation from courts, companies and sponsors \[[60](#o60)\]. The family can also write to the Ministry, aswelfare@probashi.gov.bd, which writes to the mission and the Board within 7 working days \[[239](#o239)\].

   > **Need to know**
   > - Is any compensation paid to the families of Bangladeshis killed in the UAE by the 2026 Iranian strikes, such as the water-delivery driver Ahmad Ali \[[N74](#n74)\]?

### If you lose the job

You qualify if you subscribed for 12 months in a row, paid every premium, were not resigning or dismissed for discipline, have no open absence report, and are still in the UAE \[[38](#o38)\].

You'll need \[[265](#o265), [266](#o266)\]:
- Emirates ID and unified or work permit number;
- a UAE mobile number and date of birth;
- contract, termination letter, residence cancellation, bank statement, any labour complaint.

1. Cancel the work permit first, as ILOE advises \[[265](#o265)\].
2. Within 30 days of the job ending (or of the labour dispute ending), go to diniloe.ae/nsure ("Submit your claim" on iloe.ae), the ILOE app, or call 600599555 \[[38](#o38), [120](#o120)\].
3. Log in with Emirates ID and mobile, enter the OTP, click Claim Submission, confirm the date and reason, upload papers \[[266](#o266)\].
4. Choose bank transfer (IBAN) or an exchange house \[[266](#o266)\].

What you get back: 60% of your average basic salary of the last 6 months, up to AED 10,000 a month, for up to 3 months, paid within two weeks \[[265](#o265), [38](#o38)\]; stops if you start a new job; at most 12 monthly payments in a working life \[[38](#o38), [267](#o267)\].

> **Need to know**
> - What share of ILOE claims by low-wage workers are paid, and what are the common rejection reasons?

**Insurance:** coming home within 6 months of going, after losing the job, pays Tk 50,000 to workers who left with clearance from 1 January 2023 \[[254](#o254), [242](#o242), [57](#o57)\]; see "Claiming the insurance".

### Claiming the insurance

1. **Call 16135** (+8809610102030 from abroad); the Welfare Board helps claim from Jiban Bima Corporation, the state life insurer \[[244](#o244), [68](#o68)\].
2. **Apply.** For a death, nothing is needed if the body came through the airport desk; after burial abroad or a death after return, apply at portal.wewb.gov.bd or at the Board \[[242](#o242)\]. For disability or a lost job, apply to the Board's welfare section \[[242](#o242)\]:
   - disability: Deputy Director, +88 02 48317085, 01321-158354, dd.welfare2@wewb.gov.bd;
   - lost job: Assistant Director, 01712-107301, jahanwewb@gmail.com.
3. **You'll need:** proof of clearance or membership and a passport copy, with departure and arrival stamps for a lost job; for disability, medical papers and a 6R colour photo of the disabled part of the body \[[244](#o244), [242](#o242)\]. Free; 30 working days \[[242](#o242)\].
4. **If it stalls,** complain in this order \[[242](#o242)\]:
   1. the Board's grievance officer, +88 02 55138267, 01684-501891, azizwelfare@gmail.com (30 working days);
   2. the Ministry's Joint Secretary (Administration), +88 02 41030261, jsadmin@probashi.gov.bd (20 working days);
   3. grs.gov.bd, the Cabinet Division's grievance system.

## Coming home

- **Cancel the visa properly.** The employer cancels the residence permit through ICP (AED 50 application plus AED 100 smart-services fee); a domestic worker's residence is cancelled through MOHRE or the Domestic Work Bundle \[[34](#o34)\]. Within 14 days of the job ending the employer must pay all wages, unused leave and gratuity (Leaving at the end of the job) \[[122](#o122), [113](#o113)\]. The Dubai Consulate has a "Final Settlement" form and a complaint format on bcgdubai.gov.bd, "Download Forms" \[[229 (2024)](#o229)\]. After cancellation you have the grace period in "Going home without valid papers" to leave \[[34](#o34)\].
- **Check for bans before you buy the ticket:** travel bans (Dubai Police service, Going home without valid papers, Step 2) and fines \[[234](#o234), [232](#o232)\].
- **Leaving the UAE with cash.** Declare more than AED 60,000 (about Tk 20.4 lakh) in cash, other currencies, cheques, gold or precious stones to ICP, online at declare.customs.ae or on the Afseh app \[[268](#o268)\].
- **Customs in Bangladesh.**
  - Duty-free: 65 kg of baggage, 2 used phones and 1 new phone a year, and once a year, if declared, 100 g of gold or 200 g of silver jewellery (no more than 12 pieces of one type) \[[269](#o269)\].
  - Once a year you may bring a declared gold bar up to 117 g (10 tola; duty Tk 5,000 per tola) or a silver bar up to 234 g; gold over 117 g is confiscated, and a passenger carrying undeclared gold is arrested and faces a criminal case \[[269](#o269), [270](#o270)\].
  - No alcohol on a Bangladeshi passport \[[269](#o269)\].
  - Declare foreign currency over USD 10,000 on the FMJ form \[[270](#o270)\].
  - Customs emergency line: +88 01319-056591 \[[270](#o270)\].
- **Airport help.** The Welfare Board's desks at Dhaka, Chattogram and Sylhet airports give food and water to people returning from jail or on an outpass, money to workers coming back from war, disaster or lay-off, help with customs and lost luggage, and take complaints of abuse or cheating \[[263](#o263)\]. Women returning within a year after abuse or fraud get Tk 5,000 cash \[[50](#o50)\]. Dhaka desk +8802 8901040, 01404 413388; Chattogram 01913631797; Sylhet 01558277904 \[[253](#o253), [50](#o50)\].
- **A place to stay.** The Wage Earners' Centre (Lonjonipara, Borua, Khilkhet, Dhaka-1229, near Shahjalal airport): Tk 100 admission, Tk 200 a night; book at wec.wewb.gov.bd or in person with proof of clearance or membership, a passport copy and the ticket; 01310 350555, 01754 715720 (24 hours) \[[271](#o271)\]. The Probashi Lounge at Shahjalal airport gives free wifi and phone calls and food at up to 30% off with proof of clearance or membership \[[272](#o272)\].
- **If your family needs legal help in Bangladesh, such as protecting its property,** apply to the Welfare Board (directly, through the Deputy Commissioner's expatriate welfare branch or the DEMO); it writes to the local administration \[[273](#o273), [217](#o217)\].

> **Need to know**
> - Does Dhaka airport immigration treat a returnee on a travel permit (outpass) differently, and do they keep the permit?

- **Business loan:** PKB's rehabilitation loan, up to Tk 50 lakh (Tk 3 lakh without collateral), 9% over up to 10 years, at the branch for the project's area with a trade licence and return papers \[[274](#o274)\]; fees 0.5% processing (max Tk 15,000) and 0.5% documentation \[[274](#o274)\].
- **BRAC** gives returnees airport help, counselling, money or materials to restart earning, and legal help \[[275](#o275)\].
